By directly incorporating operational constraints into the forecasting procedure, the proposed method provides a framework suitable for workforce allocation in construction practices.

</details>


### [172] [A Label-Free Physics-to-Data Acceleration Framework for Parametric Time-Dependent PDEs with Latent-Space Differential-Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05554)
*Hongjiang Wang,Weizhe Wang,Yingzheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出PHD-SF，一个统一的深度算子网络框架，通过SD-TPD分离策略与微分算子学习，在PIDON、HIDON和DIDON三种模式下实现时间依赖参数化PDE的无标签、可继承、可扩展的加速求解，仅需一个或两个代表性参数案例即可实现跨参数推断，总端到端成本低于传统PINN在单参数案例上的训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的PDE加速方法沿"物理到数据"谱系发展，物理信息求解器物理一致性强但计算成本高，数据驱动代理模型推理效率高但依赖大规模高保真数据集。两类方法大多孤立发展，仅通过显式解场标签连接，缺乏统一框架来整合两者的优势。



Method: PHD-SF提出一个紧凑的物理到数据谱系框架，核心包括：(1) SD-TPD分离策略，将空间特征、潜在动力学特征和空间微分算子分离；(2) 微分算子学习，使模型信息可复用；(3) 基于统一DON（深度算子网络）架构的三种运行模式：PIDON（物理信息模式）、HIDON（超约简化模式）和DIDON（直接推断模式）；(4) 建立分离求解、超约简化式增强、直接推断的加速路径，无需预计算的全局解场标签或显式标签传递。

Result: 在四个基准时间依赖PDE上，PHD-SF仅需在初始物理信息阶段使用一个或两个代表性参数案例即可实现准确的跨参数求解和直接推断。总端到端成本低于训练单个参数案例的传统PINN，同时支持可复用的跨参数推断。

Conclusion: PHD-SF提供了一条高效的多查询参数化PDE求解路径，显著降低了对大规模高保真数据生成及其离线成本的依赖，在物理一致性和计算效率之间实现有效平衡。

Abstract: Efficient solution of time-dependent parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) is central to computational science and engineering. Existing deep-learning-based accelerators span a physics-to-data spectrum, from physics-informed solvers with strong physical consistency but high computational cost to data-driven surrogates with efficient inference but strong dependence on high-fidelity datasets. These methods have largely evolved in isolation and are often connected only through explicit solution-field labels. We propose PHD-SF, a compact physics-to-data spectrum framework for accelerating time-dependent parametric PDEs. By combining an SD-TPD separation strategy with differential-operator learning, PHD-SF allows reusable model information, including spatial features, latent dynamical features, and spatial differential operators, to be generated, inherited, and enriched across three operating modes, PIDON, HIDON, and DIDON, within a unified DON-based architecture. This design enables label-free spectrum modeling without precomputed full-field solution labels or explicit label transfer among modes. PHD-SF further establishes a separated-solving, hyper-reduction-like enrichment, and direct-inference acceleration path, reducing the dependence on large-scale high-fidelity data generation and its offline cost. Results on four benchmark time-dependent PDEs show accurate cross-parameter solution and direct inference using only one or two representative parameter cases in the initial physics-informed stage. The total end-to-end cost is lower than that required to train a conventional PINN for a single parameter case, while supporting reusable cross-parameter inference. PHD-SF therefore provides an efficient solution path for many-query parametric PDEs.

</details>


### [173] [A hybrid s-version isogeometric strategy for dynamic crack propagation in 2D and 3D problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05589)
*Tianyu He,Kosei Kurosaki,Naoki Morita,Naoto Mitsume,Kazuki Shibanuma*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出一种混合s版本等几何分析（hS-IGA）策略，用于动态裂纹扩展分析中近裂纹断裂量的精确高效评估。该策略在全局离散化中引入B样条基函数，同时在裂纹域保留基于Lagrange的局部网格，消除了传统s方法中全局-局部耦合积分的连续性瓶颈，减少了约81%（二维）和95.6%（三维）的积分点数量，同时保持裂纹表示和DSIF后处理的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于Lagrange的s方法在全局-局部耦合积分中存在连续性相关的瓶颈，导致耦合积分的数值处理复杂且计算成本高昂；同时，裂纹表示和动态应力强度因子（DSIF）及局部应力的后处理需要保留基于Lagrange的局部网格。因此需要一种混合策略，既利用B样条基函数改善全局离散的连续性，又保留Lagrange局部网格处理裂纹的优势。

Method: 提出hS-IGA策略，保留传统s方法的全局-局部叠加框架，但在全局离散化中使用B样条基函数，局部裂纹域保持Lagrange基函数的网格。该混合公式消除了由全局近似引起的耦合积分不连续性，使得标准Gauss积分无需递归细分即可实现精确的耦合积分。通过二维静止和动态直线裂纹问题与标准有限元法和传统s方法对比验证，并通过三维静止和动态扩展圆形裂纹问题与传统s方法对比评估。

Result: hS-IGA策略能够精确评估DSIF和局部应力，同时保留s方法的全局-局部建模优势。耦合积分所需积分点数量大幅减少：相对于传统s方法，二维动态基准测试减少约81%，三维动态基准测试减少约95.6%。

Conclusion: 所提出的hS-IGA框架为需要可靠评估近裂纹断裂量的动态裂纹扩展分析提供了一种精确且高效的全局-局部策略，兼具计算效率与数值精度。

Abstract: A hybrid s-version of isogeometric analysis (hS-IGA) strategy is proposed for accurate and efficient evaluation of near-crack fracture quantities in dynamic crack propagation analysis. The strategy retains the global-local superposition framework of the conventional s-method, while introducing B-spline basis functions only into the global discretisation and preserving a Lagrange-based local mesh in the crack domain. This hybrid formulation is motivated by the continuity-related bottleneck in the global-local coupling integration of the conventional Lagrange-based s-method, and by the need to retain a Lagrange-based local mesh for crack representation and post-processing of the dynamic stress intensity factor (DSIF) and local stress. The resulting formulation removes discontinuities in the coupling integrands caused by the global approximation and enables accurate coupling integration by standard Gauss quadrature without recursive subdivision. The proposed strategy is verified using two-dimensional stationary and dynamic straight-crack problems against the standard finite element method and the conventional s-method, and is further assessed using three-dimensional stationary and dynamically propagating circular-crack problems against the conventional s-method. Results show that the proposed hS-IGA strategy accurately evaluates the DSIF and local stress while retaining the global-local modelling advantages of the s-method. It also substantially reduces the number of integration points required for coupling integration, by approximately 81% in the two-dimensional dynamic benchmark and 95.6% in the three-dimensional dynamic benchmark relative to the conventional s-method. These results demonstrate that the proposed hS-IGA framework provides an accurate and efficient global-local strategy for dynamic crack propagation analyses requiring reliable evaluation of near-crack fracture quantities.

</details>


### [174] [Benefits of Shifting Passenger Traffic from Air to Rail: A Case Study of California High-Speed Rail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05636)
*Kaijing Ding,Lu Dai,Mark Hansen*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量化的方法，从航班延误成本降低的角度评估将客运交通从航空转向高铁带来的收益。以加州高铁2020年商业计划中的客流预测为基础，评估了2029年和2033年因高铁分流航空旅客而减少的航班延误成本，分别为5100万-8800万美元和2.35亿-3.92亿美元（2018年美元）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在量化高铁替代航空出行的经济收益，特别是从航班延误成本下降这一独特视角切入，为交通基础设施投资决策和政策评估提供实证依据。

Method: 首先基于加州高铁2020商业计划的客流预测，估算各机场起降对（O-D pair）的航班削减数量，并将航班削减分配到15分钟时间间隔；随后采用Lasso模型估计SFO、LAX、SAN三个机场排队延误减少对美国Core 29国家机场到达延误的影响；最后利用飞机运营成本和旅客时间价值将延误减少货币化。研究还评估了不同的机场容量和航班时刻表情景，以及高铁客流预测概率分布的多百分位数。

Result: 研究估算得出：2029年航班延误成本节省为5100万-8800万美元（2018年美元），2033年为2.35亿-3.92亿美元（2018年美元）。

Conclusion: 高铁分担航空客流能够显著降低航班延误成本，且随着时间推移（从2029年到2033年）节约效应大幅增长；低成本航班延误的节省对整体交通基础设施投资具有正向经济价值。

Abstract: This study provides a method to quantify the benefits of shifting passenger traffic from air to high-speed rail from the perspective of flight-delay cost reduction. We first estimate the number of flight reductions for airport origin-destination pairs based on the high-speed rail ridership forecasts provided in the California High-Speed Rail 2020 Business Plan, and then distribute these flight reductions to quarter-hour intervals. Lasso models are applied to estimate the impact of reduced queuing delays at SFO, LAX, and SAN on arrival delays at the national Core 29 airports. These delay reductions are then monetized using aircraft operating costs and the value of passenger time. We evaluate alternative airport-capacity and flight-schedule scenarios, as well as multiple percentiles of probabilistic high-speed rail ridership forecasts. The resulting estimates indicate flight-delay cost savings of $51-88 million in 2018 dollars in 2029 and $235-392 million in 2018 dollars in 2033.

</details>


### [175] [Hyperelastic Membranes with Implicitly Defined, Continuously Embedded Fibers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05717)
*Michael Wolfgang Kaiser,Thomas-Peter Fries*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于各向异性、超弹性曲面膜的新型力学模型及对应的有限元方法。超弹性纤维嵌入各向同性膜中，通过切向微分计算的坐标无关框架实现几何非线性的隐式纤维描述，并结合曲面有限元与虚拟域方法进行数值求解，获得了高阶收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物组织和纺织品等材料可被简化为带有嵌入纤维的各向异性曲面膜模型，但现有方法在描述纤维几何和耦合力学方面存在局限，需要一种基于连续介质力学第一性原理的、坐标无关的鲁棒建模与数值求解方法。

Method: 采用有限应变理论（几何非线性）建立曲面膜的力学方程；通过切向微分计算（tangential differential calculus）以坐标无关的方式定义微分算子；纤维几何通过标量函数的水平集与显式曲面相交进行（半）隐式描述；将隐式纤维的力学模型与膜力学耦合；数值上采用曲面有限元与虚拟域方法的混合方案。

Result: 对于光滑物理场，数值方法获得了高阶收敛率，证实了所提出的数值方法及整体建模框架的有效性与成功性。

Conclusion: 该混合曲面有限元/虚拟域方法为各向异性超弹性曲面膜（如生物组织、纺织品）提供了一种基于第一性原理且几何灵活的高精度数值求解框架，兼具理论严谨性与计算可行性。

Abstract: A novel mechanical model and corresponding finite element method for anisotropic, hyperelastic, curved membranes are proposed. Hyperelastic fibers are embedded into the otherwise isotropic membrane, being relevant, for example, in reduced models for biological tissues and textiles. The geometrically nonlinear mechanics is formulated based on first principles of continuum mechanics (finite strain theory). The employed differential operators are formulated in a coordinate-free manner, through a framework known as tangential differential calculus. This enables a (semi-)implicit description of the fiber geometry through the intersection of level sets of some scalar function with the explicitly defined membrane surface. The mechanical model of the implicit fibers is then coupled to the mechanics of the membrane. For the numerical analysis, finite elements are applied such that the resulting scheme is a hybrid between classical Surface FEM and fictitious domain methods. For smooth physical fields, higher-order convergence rates are obtained and confirm the success of the numerical method.

</details>


### [176] [Accurate simulation of delamination with a resin-rich layer-dependent penalty stiffness based on structural cohesive elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05881)
*Xiaopeng Ai,Christos Kassapoglou,Boyang Chen*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究将之前开发的高阶结构粘聚单元与依赖于树脂富集层的罚刚度相结合，用以改善裂尖前方压缩分布的预测精度和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于壳的粘聚单元因罚刚度的近似处理，往往会高估裂尖前方的压缩应力，导致预测不准。

Method: 区分法向和剪切罚刚度，将树脂富集层罚刚度从逐层框架扩展到等效单层框架，利用梁理论推导出的面外法向和横向剪应力的厚度方向分布来实现该扩展。

Result: 在Mode I、Mode II、混合模式和增强DCB构型的基准问题上进行了验证，与传统公式相比，网格收敛性显著改善，裂尖前方压缩分布和分层扩展预测更准确。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法同时提升了计算效率和预测精度，为层合板分层模拟提供了一种更可靠的粘聚单元方案。

Abstract: Shell-based cohesive elements tend to overestimate the compression ahead of the crack tip because of approximations in the penalty stiffness. In this study, the higher-order structural cohesive element previously developed by the authors is enhanced with a resin-rich layer-dependent penalty stiffness to improve both computational efficiency and predictive accuracy. The proposed formulation distinguishes between the normal and shear penalty stiffnesses. It extends the resin-rich layer-based penalty stiffness from the layer-wise to the equivalent single-layer framework. This extension is achieved using the through-thickness distributions of the out-of-plane normal and transverse shear stresses derived from beam theory. The proposed method is verified and validated against benchmark problems for Mode I, Mode II, mixed-mode, and reinforced DCB configurations. Compared with the conventional formulation, it exhibits significantly improved mesh convergence and provides more accurate predictions of the compression distribution ahead of the crack tip and the delamination propagation.

</details>


### [177] [Quantitative Analysis of Media Bias and Stock Price Dynamics: The 2020 Shock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05899)
*Shivansh Verma,Soham Tulsyan,Sashwat Dhanuka,Anirban Sen*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文利用2015至2025年间26家美国大型企业的628万条新闻标题，研究金融新闻与股价之间的关系是否在COVID-19大流行冲击下发生变化。通过构建逐日立场指标，并运用面板回归和数据驱动结构断点的向量自回归模型，发现媒体与市场之间的动态关系主要在个别企业自身结构断点附近出现，而非整体市场层面，表明二者互动关系具有显著的企业特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融新闻究竟是影响股价还是仅仅反映已被市场吸收的信息，是金融经济学中尚未解决的关键问题。COVID-19大流行同时大规模扰乱了新闻报道和金融市场，为重新审视这一问题提供了独特契机。现有研究多依赖总体情绪指标，无法判断观察到的关系是否在个体企业层面同样成立。

Method: 收集2015至2025年间26家美国大型企业的628万条新闻标题，过滤出具有实质性相关的企业特定报道后，构建逐日媒体立场指标。采用面板回归和数据驱动结构断点（structural breaks）的向量自回归（VAR）模型，分析媒体立场与股票收益之间的关系在2020年冲击前后的变化。

Result: 研究发现金融新闻与股票市场之间的关系远比总体分析所显示的更为复杂。在大流行之后，未发现媒体立场或股票收益存在持续的市场整体性变化；但对于一部分企业，在其自身结构断点附近出现了显著的动态关系。

Conclusion: 理解媒体与市场之间的互动需要对个别企业进行特异性分析，本研究为在变化的市场条件下研究新闻与价格如何相互作用提供了一个分析框架。

Abstract: Whether financial news influences stock prices or simply reflects information already incorporated into them remains an open question in financial economics. The COVID-19 pandemic provides an opportunity to revisit this question, as it disrupted both news coverage and financial markets on an unprecedented scale. Existing studies have largely approached the problem through aggregate sentiment measures, leaving it unclear whether the observed relationships also hold at the level of individual firms. We study this question using 6.28 million news headlines covering 26 large United States firms between 2015 and 2025. After filtering the corpus to retain materially relevant firm-specific coverage, we construct daily stance measures and examine how their relationship with stock returns changed around the 2020 shock using panel regressions and vector autoregressions with data-driven structural breaks. Our findings indicate that the relationship between financial news and equity markets is more nuanced than aggregate analyses alone suggest. While we find little evidence of a persistent market-wide change in media stance or stock returns following the pandemic, dynamic relationships emerge for a subset of firms around their own structural breaks. Taken together, these results suggest that understanding media-market interactions requires firm specific analysis and provide a framework for studying how news and prices interact under changing market conditions.

</details>


### [178] [Local-Global Feature Mixer and Trend-Guided Consistent Learning for Remaining Useful Life Prediction of Rotating Machinery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05925)
*Hanbyeol Park,Hyerim Bae*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 该研究提出局部-全局特征混合器（LGFM）和趋势引导的循环一致性（TG-RC）损失函数，用于解决递归健康指标（HI）预测中的误差累积和长期退化趋势保持问题，在两个公开轴承数据集上验证了其在长期外推稳定性和RUL预测性能上的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退化过程（DP）建模广泛用于剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测，尤其是在run-to-failure数据有限的情况下。神经网络虽然能描绘复杂退化轨迹，但递归健康指标预测容易产生误差累积，且难以在长期预测中保持不可逆的退化趋势。

Method: 提出LGFM将原始HI序列与统计特征和退化相关特征相结合，降低高频噪声敏感性并同时捕获局部变化与全局退化状态；提出TG-RC损失，在传统一步均方误差基础上补充递归多步展开损失和基于全局趋势先验的软动态时间规整（soft-DTW）对齐项，减少训练与递归推理之间的差异，引导预测轨迹朝向一致的退化方向。

Result: 在两个公开轴承数据集上的实验表明，该框架在不同检测时间下提升了长期HI外推稳定性和RUL预测性能；LGFM保持低计算复杂度，TG-RC损失可集成到多种DP模型中提升其长期预测性能。

Conclusion: 通过LGFM与TG-RC损失的结合，有效解决了递归HI预测中的误差累积和退化趋势保持问题，且具有较好的通用性和计算效率。

Abstract: Degradation process (DP) modeling is widely used for remaining useful life (RUL) prediction, particularly when run-to-failure data are limited. Neural networks can present complex degradation trajectories without prescribing a fixed degradation function; however, recursive health indicator (HI) forecasting is prone to error accumulation and may fail to preserve the irreversible degradation trend over long horizons. To address these limitations, this study proposes a local-global feature mixer (LGFM) and trend-guided rollout-consistent (TG-RC) loss. The LGFM combines the original HI sequence with statistical and degradation-related features to reduce sensitivity to high-frequency noise and capture both local changes and global degradation states. The TG-RC loss supplements the conventional one-step mean squared error with a recursive multi-step rollout loss and a soft dynamic time warping alignment term based on a global trend prior. Consequently, it reduces the discrepancy between training and recursive inference, while guiding the predicted trajectory toward a consistent degradation direction. Experiments on two public bearing datasets show that the proposed framework improves long-term HI extrapolation stability and RUL prediction performance across different inspection times. The LGFM also maintains low computational complexity, while the TG-RC loss can be incorporated into various DP models to improve their long-term forecasting performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Agentic Nesting: A New Methodology for Existing Enterprise Application Integration and Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05159)
*Xi Wang,Kun Li,Xianyao Ling,Gang Yin,Liang Zhang,Jiang Wu,Wenbo Lei,Jun Xu,Annie Wang,Fu Zhang,Weizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Nesting，一种多智能体协作框架，将现有企业应用封装为自主AI智能体并组织成分层嵌套结构，通过中央编排器进行任务分解与动态调度，实现跨应用的自然语言交互和流程编排，提出"应用即智能体"和"对话即集成"的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业运营依赖多个异构业务系统和信息应用，导致严重的数据孤岛和流程碎片化。传统企业应用集成方法（如ESB中间件、API网关、RPA）存在高架构耦合、运维成本上升和智能能力有限等固有缺陷，亟需新的集成范式。

Method: 提出Agentic Nesting多智能体协作框架：1）将现有企业应用封装为自主AI智能体，提取数字代理以实现自然语言交互和自主操作；2）智能体按分层托管拓扑组织，镜像企业生态系统的组合复杂性，而非扁平互连；3）通过中央编排器协调多个智能体进行任务分解和动态调度；4）提供统一对话接口用于跨应用查询和流程编排。

Result: 主要贡献包括："应用即智能体"（Application-as-Agent）集成范式、"对话即集成"（Conversation-as-Integration）交互哲学，以及在异构系统协调和大规模数据应用场景中该方法的泛化潜力探索。

Conclusion: Agentic Nesting为企业应用集成提供了一种以AI智能体和自然语言对话为核心的新范式，有望克服传统集成方法在耦合性、运维成本和智能化方面的局限，实现对企业既有应用资产的有效编排与利用。

Abstract: Enterprise operations extensively rely on multiple heterogeneous business systems and information applications, which also result in severe data silos and process fragmentation. Enterprises have invested considerable financial and material resources in building these applications, however, effectively leveraging and orchestrating them remains a formidable challenge. Conventional approaches to enterprise application integration, encompassing middleware architectures such as Enterprise Service Bus (ESB), API gateway infrastructures, and Robotic Process Automation (RPA), suffer from inherent limitations like high architectural coupling, escalating operation and maintenance costs, and limited intelligence capabilities. This paper proposes Agentic Nesting, a multi-agent collaboration framework in which existing enterprise applications are encapsulated as autonomous AI agents within a hierarchically nested structure. Rather than flat interconnection, agents are organized into layered stewardship topologies that mirror the compositional complexity of enterprise ecosystems. The framework extracts a digital agent proxy from each legacy application to enable natural-language interaction and autonomous manipulation, coordinates multiple agents through a central orchestrator for task decomposition and dynamic dispatching, and exposes a unified conversational interface for cross-application querying and process orchestration. The main contributions of this paper are the proposition of the "Application-as-Agent" integration paradigm and the "Conversation-as-Integration" interaction philosophy, together with an exploration of the generalization potential of this methodology in scenarios encompassing heterogeneous system coordination, and large-scale data applications.

</details>


### [180] [The Ignition Index: Measuring Global Workspace Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05160)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ignition Index（燃点指数）——一个将全局工作空间理论（GWT）的"全有或全无"点火预测操作化到Transformer语言模型中的标量度量。该指标通过拟合四参数Sigmoid曲线到逐层线性探针准确率与输入信号强度的关系，提取陡峭度参数β̂。在11个模型、5个架构家族上的实验表明，该指标对真实语言结构具有9.6倍选择性（p<0.001），并揭示了前馈Transformer与SSM之间的显著差异（89%聚合β̂差异），以及循环架构沿迭代轴的类工作空间转换等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局工作空间理论（GWT）预言意识相关的神经活动具有"全有或全无"的点火特性，但这一动力学预测在人工神经网络中缺乏定量验证。现有可解释性研究缺乏一个能与GWT预测直接对接的、经过验证的定量指标，以衡量语言模型中信息处理是呈现阶跃式（ignition-like）还是渐进式（graded）的特征转换。

Method: 1) 对每个模型层的线性探针准确率（作为信号强度的函数）拟合四参数Sigmoid曲线；2) 提取陡峭度参数β̂作为点火指数（Ignition Index）；3) 采用shuffled-label对照实验验证指标选择性（9.6倍选择性）；4) 在11个模型、5个架构家族（前馈Transformer、SSM、循环架构等）中进行系统比较；5) 使用Mann-Whitney U检验、Cohen's d等统计方法验证差异显著性；6) 使用PELT算法检测训练过程中的相变。

Result: 1) Ignition Index具有9.6倍的真实结构选择性（p<0.001）；2) 前馈Transformer的聚合β̂比SSM高89%（p<1e-13, Cohen's d=0.52），Mamba呈近线性轮廓，缺乏全局广播；3) Huginn-3.5B沿迭代轴的点燃强度是深度轴的2.12倍，说明循环架构沿循环维度展现工作空间式转换；4) Pythia-410M在训练第256步检测到PELT相变（+67%），先于induction-head形成；5) 点火与模型规模、信号强度相关的假设未获支持，推测Transformer架构可能已饱和可用点火机制。

Conclusion: Ignition Index为GWT的动力学预测与机制可解释性之间提供了首个经过验证的定量桥梁，具有9.6倍测量选择性和此前缩放定律文献中未表征的架构级判别力，为理解不同架构（Transformer vs. SSM vs. 循环）中信息处理的本质差异提供了新的理论工具和实证基础。

Abstract: We introduce the Ignition Index (I), a validated scalar metric that operationalizes Global Workspace Theory's (GWT) all-or-none ignition prediction in transformer language models. The metric fits a four-parameter sigmoid to per-layer linear probe accuracy as a function of input signal strength, extracting steepness parameter beta-hat: high values indicate abrupt, ignition-like transitions; low values indicate graded build-up. Across 11 models spanning five architecture families, shuffled-label controls demonstrate 9.6-fold selectivity for genuine linguistic structure over spurious probe capacity (p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U-test). We find: (1) Feedforward transformers exceed SSMs by 89% in aggregate beta-hat (p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52), with Mamba exhibiting near-linear profiles consistent with absent global broadcast. (2) Huginn-3.5B exhibits 2.12-fold higher ignition along its iteration axis than its depth axis, demonstrating that recurrent architectures manifest workspace-like transitions along the recurrence dimension. (3) Pythia-410M shows a PELT-detected phase transition at training step 256 (+67%), preceding induction-head formation. (4) Hypotheses linking ignition to model scale and signal strength were not confirmed, suggesting transformer architectures may saturate available ignition mechanisms. The Ignition Index provides the first validated quantitative bridge between GWT's dynamical predictions and mechanistic interpretability, with 9.6-fold measurement selectivity and architecture-level discriminability not previously characterized in the scaling literature. Code: https://github.com/saman-rahbar/ignition-index

</details>


### [181] [Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yang Li,Deguo Xia,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Woodpecker Distillation框架，通过对比弱模型在同一推理前缀上成功与不成功的局部干预补丁，构建矫正性教师分布并将其蒸馏到强模型中，从而修复LLM在推理任务中的局部推理错误，在数学推理基准上优于直接模仿基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理任务中经常失败，作者认为这些失败源于中间步骤的局部推理错误而非整体能力不足。这些错误可通过插入短补丁修复，但直接对修复轨迹进行微调无法内化这一矫正效果，因为有用的信号不在于干预文本本身，而在于它如何重塑模型未来的推理分布。因此需要一种新的训练框架来捕捉这种信号。

Method: Woodpecker Distillation框架：(1) 在相同推理前缀下，让弱探测模型生成成功和不成功的局部干预补丁；(2) 对比这两类补丁诱导的未来token预测，构建矫正性教师分布；(3) 将此信号蒸馏到强模型中，使其内化矫正后的推理路径。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，Woodpecker Distillation持续提升强模型性能，并优于直接模仿（直接对弱补丁或修复轨迹进行微调）的基线方法。

Conclusion: 局部推理错误是可修复的，但关键的矫正信号在于干预如何重塑未来推理分布，而非干预文本本身。通过对比性局部干预的蒸馏学习，可以有效利用弱模型的矫正信号来提升强模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models often fail on reasoning tasks despite possessing the capability to solve them. We argue that many such failures arise from localized reasoning bugs in intermediate steps rather than from global incompetence. We show that these bugs are frequently repairable: inserting a short patch generated by a weak probe model after the same strong-model reasoning prefix can redirect the trajectory toward a correct solution.
  However, this corrective effect is not reliably internalized by directly fine-tuning on weak patches or repaired trajectories, suggesting that the useful signal lies not in the intervention text itself, but in how it reshapes the model's future reasoning distribution. We therefore propose Woodpecker Distillation, a weak-to-strong training framework that learns from contrastive local interventions. Our method contrasts successful and unsuccessful weak-model patches at the same prefix, constructs a corrective teacher distribution from their induced future token predictions, and distills this signal into the strong model.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Woodpecker Distillation consistently improves strong-model performance and outperforms direct imitation baselines.

</details>


### [182] [From Continuous Predictors to Clinical Thresholds: Early Evidence on Performance Trade-offs of Guideline-Based Categorisation for Ischaemic Stroke Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05203)
*Esra Zihni,Katryna Cisek,Hamzah Ziadeh,Hendrik Knoche,Robert Mikulik,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨在急性缺血性卒中90天结局预测模型中，用临床指南对齐的分类编码替代连续预测变量是否会影响模型性能。研究发现，在三个治疗队列中的两个，完全分类化模型与连续变量模型在预测准确性上无显著差异，且全局特征重要性排序保持一致，表明指南导向的分类化是卒中结局模型可行的设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在急性缺血性卒中的90天结局预测中已取得高预测精度，但临床采用受限，因为模型解释与临床医生的推理方式不一致。临床医生用户研究呼吁采用与临床指南对齐的截断值，因此需要验证是否可以用临床信息化的分类编码替代连续预测变量而不牺牲性能。

Method: 使用多中心欧洲登记数据，将患者分为三个治疗队列。比较标准梯度提升机模型与完全分类化梯度提升机模型，后者采用卒中指南对齐的、按治疗特异的阈值对连续变量进行离散化。通过统计分析评估两模型在预测准确性上的差异，并比较全局特征重要性排序。

Result: 在三个治疗队列中，完全分类化模型在两个队列中与连续变量模型在预测准确性上统计上不可区分；在一个队列中出现显著掉点。全局特征重要性排名在所有治疗组中保持一致，说明离散化保留了预后因素的核心层级结构。

Conclusion: 基于指南的分类化是卒中结局模型可行的设计选择，能在保持预测性能和特征重要性层级的同时，使模型解释更贴合临床医生的推理方式，从而有望促进临床采纳。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve strong predictive accuracy for 90-day outcome prediction in acute ischaemic stroke, yet clinical adoption is limited by the misalignment of model explanations with clinicians' reasoning. Motivated by a clinician user study calling for clinical guideline-aligned cut-offs, we ask whether continuous predictors can be replaced by clinically informed categorical encodings without sacrificing performance. On a multi-centre European registry stratified into three treatment cohorts, we compare standard and fully categorised gradient-boosted models, the latter using stroke guideline-aligned, treatment-specific thresholds. The fully categorised models are statistically indistinguishable from their continuous counterparts in two of the treatment cohorts, with a significant drop in predictive accuracy in one cohort. Global feature importance rankings remain consistent, suggesting that discretising continuous predictors into guideline-based categories preserves the core hierarchy of prognostic factors across all treatment groups. Guideline-based categorisation is thus a viable design choice for stroke-outcome models.

</details>


### [183] [SkillTrace: Multi-Trace Provenance Auditing for LLM-Agent Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05204)
*Jialuo Chen,Minghe Wang,Lingqi Jiang,Jianan Ma,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yunhao Feng,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SKILLTRACE是一个面向LLM-agent技能复用的多轨迹溯源审计框架，通过提取表达、实现和操作三条溯源轨迹（其中操作轨迹用技能操作图SOG表示），在审计时确定性比较缓存轨迹并校准，在SKILLTRACE-BENCH基准上达到AUROC 0.938和F1 0.898，并在36,446个技能的野外审计中证明了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-agent生态系统围绕可复用技能快速增长，技能成为市场商品后，审计其复用不再等同于普通代码克隆检测。现有检测器只针对单一模态源代码或整个包相似性，无法捕捉仅保留技能某一部分的复用证据，因为技能复用证据分布在作者文本、实现片段和操作结构中。

Method: SKILLTRACE提取三条溯源轨迹：表达轨迹(Expression)、实现轨迹(Implementation)和操作轨迹(Operational)。操作轨迹被表示为技能操作图(SOG)，捕获激活、流程和资源流结构。LLM仅在摄取时辅助操作轨迹提取一次，审计时对缓存的轨迹进行确定性比较，针对同功能严格负样本校准每条轨迹，并报告哪条轨迹支持复用决策。

Result: 在SKILLTRACE-BENCH上（820个变换复用正样本，覆盖100个市场锚点和751个负对照），SKILLTRACE达到AUROC 0.938和F1 0.898；在36,446个技能的野外审计中，轨迹归因证据生成了超越仓库级基线的可操作复用审查队列。

Conclusion: SKILLTRACE通过多轨迹溯源审计框架有效解决了LLM-agent技能复用的审计问题，能够识别仅保留技能单一组成部分的复用行为，超越传统代码克隆检测和仓库级基线的能力。

Abstract: LLM-agent ecosystems are rapidly growing around reusable skills: mixed-modality packages of metadata, natural-language instructions, code, tools, references, and operational workflows. As skills become marketplace artifacts, auditing their reuse is no longer the same problem as ordinary code clone detection. Existing detectors target single-modality source code or whole-package similarity, yet skill reuse evidence is distributed across authored text, implementation fragments, and operational structure. As a result, they can miss reuse that preserves only one part of a skill. We present SKILLTRACE, a multi-trace provenance auditing framework for LLM-agent skill reuse. SKILLTRACE extracts three provenance traces: Expression, Implementation, and Operational. It represents the Operational Trace as a Skill Operational Graph (SOG) that captures activation, procedure, and resource-flow structure. An LLM assists only the Operational-trace extraction, once at ingestion; at audit time SKILLTRACE compares cached traces deterministically, calibrates each trace against same-function strict negatives, and reports which trace supports a reuse decision. On SKILLTRACE-BENCH, with 820 transformed reuse positives over 100 marketplace anchors and 751 negative controls, SKILLTRACE achieves AUROC 0.938 and F1 0.898. A 36,446-skill wild audit further shows that trace-attributed evidence surfaces actionable reuse review queues beyond repository-level baselines.

</details>


### [184] [Abstract Event Causal Rules: Induction and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05205)
*Ziwei Zheng,Peiqiong Chen,Bang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR），一种将具体因果对转换为泛化抽象因果逻辑的关系级抽象范式，通过多智能体CACI系统与相似度约束聚类从噪声数据中蒸馏可信AECR，并构建AR-GCAE编码器将AECR注入事件预测任务，显著提升尤其是低频长尾和未见事件组合上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件中心智能分析系统依赖显式因果事件知识，但现有实例级因果对在低频长尾和未见事件组合上存在严重泛化缺陷，需要一种更高层次（关系级）的因果抽象表示来克服这一局限。

Method: 提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR）关系级因果抽象范式；设计多智能体具体到抽象因果归纳（CACI）系统结合相似度约束聚类从噪声原始数据中蒸馏可信AECR，构建两个完整的AECR知识库；并提出抽象规则引导的因果注意力编码器（AR-GCAE），通过规则引导注意力层和门控表示融合将检索到的AECR注入因果图事件预测（CGEP）基准任务。

Result: 定量实验结果表明，应用AECR能显著增强事件因果推理的泛化能力，并为事件预测带来一致的性能提升，尤其在稀有和未见事件样本上增益最为突出。

Conclusion: AECR范式有效解决了实例级因果对泛化不足的问题，关系级抽象因果知识具有实用价值，验证了抽象因果规则在事件预测任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Event-centric intelligent analytical systems heavily depend on explicit causal event knowledge for risk early warning, decision-making support and narrative comprehension. Nevertheless, existing instance-level causal pairs suffer severe generalization deficits on low-frequency long-tail and unseen event combinations. To address this limitation, this work proposes Abstract Event Causal Rule (AECR), a novel relation-level causal abstraction paradigm that transforms concrete cause-effect pairs into generalized abstract causal logic while retaining their intrinsic causal relationships. We design a multi-agent Concrete-to-Abstract Causal Induction (CACI) system coupled with similarity-constrained clustering to distill trustworthy AECRs from noisy raw causal data, based on which two complete AECR knowledge bases are built. To validate the practical utility of abstract causal knowledge, we propose an Abstract Rule-Guided Causal Attention Encoder (AR-GCAE), which injects the retrieved AECRs into the causality Graph Event Prediction (CGEP) benchmark task via rule-guided attention layers and gated representation fusion. Quantitative experimental results reveal that applying AECRs substantially strengthens the generalization capacity of event causal reasoning and brings consistent performance improvements to event prediction, with the most prominent gains observed on rare and unseen event samples.

</details>


### [185] [Otter: A Time-Aware, History-Conditioned Human Chess AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05206)
*Tarun Kumar S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Otter是一个15.3M参数的人类国际象棋AI，通过将行棋建模为时间感知的序贯过程（结合过往20步移动历史编码器和时间内控制模块），在6.1亿个位置上训练，实现了55.23%的top-1和90.95%的top-5人类行棋预测准确率，超越此前最优的Maia 2模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人类行棋预测模型（如Maia 2）通常将每个棋盘位置视为孤立输入，忽视了游戏过程中时间语境和移动历史对人类决策的重要影响。作者旨在验证将国际象棋建模为时间感知的序贯活动，能否比仅依赖位置的模型更准确地预测人类行棋行为。

Method: Otter采用一种新架构，整合两个条件建模信号：移动历史编码器，基于最近20步棋进行条件预测，捕捉开局偏好、位置漂移和局内行为趋势；时间内控制模块，根据时钟压力调节预测。模型参数量仅15.3M，使用6.1亿个位置（来自1.17亿局Lichess快棋对局）在单个T4 GPU上训练30天。

Result: Otter在人类行棋预测任务上达到55.23%的top-1准确率和90.95%的top-5准确率，全面超越之前SOTA模型Maia 2，且参数量更少、训练数据更少。在11个Elo分档（<1100至>=2000）中，最高准确率出现在1900-1999分段，达到57.38%。

Conclusion: 建模国际象棋为时间感知、序贯进行的人类活动，比仅基于位置的建模方法能更准确地预测人类行棋。即使使用更小的模型，这种方式也能取得更好的预测表现。代码、训练好的模型以及完整训练日志已公开释放，便于复现和进一步研究。

Abstract: Otter is a 15.3M-parameter human chess AI that predicts human move selection by modeling play as a time-aware, sequential process rather than treating each position in isolation. It combines two conditioning signals: (1) a move history encoder that conditions predictions on the last 20 moves, capturing opening preferences, positional drift, and intra-game behavioral tendencies; and (2) a time control module that modulates predictions based on clock pressure. Otter is trained on 6.1 billion positions from 117 million Lichess rapid games over 30 days on a single T4 GPU.
  Otter achieves 55.23% top-1 and 90.95% top-5 move-prediction accuracy, surpassing the prior state-of-the-art human chess model, Maia 2, with far fewer parameters and less training data. Across 11 Elo brackets (<1100 to >=2000), accuracy peaks at 57.38% in the 1900-1999 bracket. These results show that modeling chess as a time-aware, sequential activity yields more human-accurate move prediction than position-only approaches, using a smaller model. Code, trained models, and complete training logs are publicly released.

</details>


### [186] [SearchAuditor: Auditing and Attributing Failures in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05212)
*Zhixiang Liang,Yifei Liu,Yidan Huang,Haozhe Zhao,Beichen Huang,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan,Qiong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SearchAuditBench基准和SearchAuditor框架，用于评估和改进LLM审计器对深度搜索智能体长轨迹失败进行定位、归因和修复的能力。SearchAuditBench包含1,243条失败轨迹（平均73.1条消息/65.1K tokens），来自8个开源模型在5个深度搜索基准上的结果，每条都经专家标注了关键错误步骤、根因和参考修复方案。SearchAuditor通过多视角证据驱动的裁决框架，在端到端通过率上达到32.3%，显著超越最强基线（GPT-5.5驱动的26.6%），且其修复能帮助智能体更好地从错误中恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索智能体在长时间交互中极易出错，且微小的推理错误会沿长噪声轨迹传播为看似流畅但错误的答案。人工诊断这些失败需要检查极长的执行轨迹，超出人类能力范围，因此需要自动化审计器来减少人工负担。

Method: 1) 构建SearchAuditBench基准：从8个开源模型在5个深度搜索基准上收集1,243条失败轨迹，逐条进行专家标注（关键错误步骤、搜索特定根因、参考修复与评分标准）。2) 提出SearchAuditor框架：采用多视角审计策略，通过证据基础的裁决来执行失败定位、根因归因和错误修复。

Result: 最强基线（GPT-5.5驱动）仅达到26.6%的端到端通过率；SearchAuditor在不同前沿模型上一致超越所有基线，达到32.3%的端到端通过率。此外，使用SearchAuditor的修复来恢复失败运行，能使智能体更好地从错误中恢复。

Conclusion: SearchAuditor有效提升了审计器对深度搜索智能体失败的定位、归因和修复能力，验证了多视角证据驱动审计框架的优越性，为减少深度搜索系统的人工诊断负担提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Deep search agents tackle challenging questions through long-horizon web interactions, a process that is both complex and fragile: small reasoning errors may propagate through long, noisy trajectories into fluent but incorrect answers. Diagnosing such failures is difficult, requiring the manual inspection of extremely long execution traces, which could be beyond human capacity. We therefore introduce SearchAuditBench, a benchmark that evaluates whether LLM auditors can localize, attribute, and repair these failures, thereby reducing the human burden. SearchAuditBench comprises 1,243 failed trajectories, averaging 73.1 messages and 65.1K tokens, collected from eight open-weight models on five deep-search benchmarks, each expert-annotated with the critical error step, a search-specific root cause, and a reference repair with grading rubrics. We further propose SearchAuditor, a multi-perspective auditing framework that effectively localizes, attributes, and repairs search-agent failures through evidence-grounded adjudication. Experimental results show that even the strongest baseline, when powered by a frontier model like GPT-5.5, attains only a 26.6% end-to-end pass rate. In contrast, our SearchAuditor consistently outperforms all baselines across different frontier models, achieving an end-to-end pass rate of 32.3%, and resuming failed runs with its repairs enables agents to better recover from errors.

</details>


### [187] [PD-GS: Phoneme-Driven 3DGS for Audio-Driven Talking Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05218)
*Ao Fu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PD-GS（Phoneme-Driven Gaussian Splatting），通过将时间对齐的音素令牌与连续的音频上下文通过可学习的门控机制融合，解决3DGS说话头渲染中嘴唇过度平滑和"漏嘴"伪影问题，在HDTF数据集上取得了最优的嘴唇几何精度（LMD 2.66）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS说话头渲染在唇部发音上存在严重缺陷：由于从连续的声学嵌入通过回归目标推断离散的发音事件，导致嘴部运动被过度平滑，并可能违反诸如双唇闭合等硬性发音约束，产生"漏嘴"伪影。现代自监督语音编码器虽然提供丰富的韵律和语音线索，但无法提供显式、帧对齐的语言学目标来可靠地区分成闭合级别的事件。

Method: 提出Phoneme-Driven Gaussian Splatting (PD-GS)框架：1) 通过自动语音识别（ASR）和强制对齐管线获取时间对齐的音素令牌；2) 核心组件Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)利用学习的门控机制自适应融合连续音频上下文与离散音素嵌入，在保留平滑音频驱动的动态的同时，强化发音关键片段上的音素引导；3) 仅使用单目视频，通过图像重建和唇部关键点监督进行训练。

Result: 在HDTF数据集上，PD-GS在所有对比基线中取得了最优的嘴唇几何精度（LMD 2.66），并在具有挑战性的音素序列中定性减少了闭合违规，生成更具语言学忠实度的神经化身。

Conclusion: 通过引入显式的帧对齐音素令牌并采用门控自适应融合机制，PD-GS有效缓解了3DGS说话头中唇部发音不准确的问题，为神经说话头渲染提供了更语言学可靠的技术路径。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables fast, photorealistic talking-head rendering, yet accurate lip articulation remains elusive: mouth motion is often over-smoothed and may violate hard articulatory constraints such as bilabial closures, producing the notorious ``leaky mouth'' artifact. A key difficulty is that brief, discrete articulatory events are inferred from a continuous acoustic embedding under a regression objective, which biases predictions toward averaged mouth configurations. While modern self-supervised speech encoders provide rich prosodic and phonetic cues, they do not provide an explicit, frame-aligned linguistic target that reliably disambiguates closure-level events. We propose \textbf{Phoneme-Driven Gaussian Splatting (PD-GS)}, which augments a 3DGS talker with time-aligned phoneme tokens obtained from an automatic ASR and forced-alignment pipeline. Our core component, the \textbf{Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)}, adaptively fuses continuous audio context with discrete phoneme embeddings through a learned gate, allowing the model to preserve smooth audio-driven dynamics while strengthening phoneme guidance on articulation-critical segments. PD-GS is trained purely from monocular video using image reconstruction and lip landmark supervision. On HDTF, PD-GS achieves the best lip geometry among the compared baselines (LMD 2.66) and qualitatively reduces closure violations in challenging phoneme sequences, yielding more linguistically faithful neural avatars.

</details>


### [188] [GSBF: Gaussian Splatting for Environment-Aware Beamforming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05896)
*Yijie Bian,Wei Guo,Zixin Wang,Shenghui Song,Jun Zhang,Khaled B. Letaief*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D高斯泼溅（3D Gaussian Splatting）的环境感知波束赋形（GSBF）方法，利用多模态数据构建持久性3D高斯环境表征，通过双向球面高斯（Bi-SG）核和电磁光栅化渲染角度传播器图，直接从AP位姿和用户位置合成恒模波束，无需在线瞬时CSI，显著降低导频开销和计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MIMO波束赋形依赖精确的瞬时CSI和迭代优化，导致大量导频开销和计算复杂度。既然无线电传播本质上由物理几何决定，可以利用环境几何信息来避免对实时CSI的依赖，从而降低系统开销和延迟。

Method: GSBF基于多模态数据构建持久性3D高斯环境表征；使用保持互易性的双向球面高斯（Bi-SG）核建模环境散射响应；执行两侧电磁栅格化（two-sided electromagnetic rasterization）渲染角度传播器图；随后将渲染图通过过完备阵列流形字典聚合，投影到恒模波束赋形器，从而直接从AP位姿和用户位置合成波束，无需在线瞬时CSI。

Result: 仿真结果表明，GSBF在性能上持续优于穷举波束对齐（EBA）等基线方法，且具有更低的延迟。

Conclusion: GSBF验证了利用环境几何信息进行环境感知波束赋形的可行性，在无需在线瞬时CSI的情况下实现优于传统穷举波束对齐的性能，同时降低延迟，为MIMO系统提供了一种高效且低开销的波束合成新范式。

Abstract: Beamforming plays a key role in multiple-input-multiple-output (MIMO) communication systems. However, conventional beamforming design normally requires accurate instantaneous channel state information (CSI) and iterative optimization, which incur substantial pilot overhead and computational complexity. Recognizing that radio propagation is intrinsically governed by the physical geometry, we develop a 3D Gaussian splatting for environment-aware beamforming (GSBF) pipeline based on multi-modal data, which characterizes the environment through a persistent 3D Gaussian representation. Specifically, GSBF models the environmental scattering response with reciprocity-preserving bidirectional spherical Gaussian (Bi-SG) kernels and performs two-sided electromagnetic rasterization to render an angular propagator map. The rendered map is then aggregated through an over-complete array-manifold dictionary and projected to the constant-modulus beamformers, thereby synthesizing beams directly from the access point (AP) pose and user position without online instantaneous CSI. Simulations demonstrate that GSBF consistently outperforms baselines such as exhaustive beam alignment (EBA) with lower latency.

</details>


### [189] [When Privileged Guidance Misaligns: State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05219)
*Junzhuo Liu,Weiwei Li,Jun Ling,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SMRC-SD方法，通过状态匹配路由和有状态上下文化的自蒸馏，解决特权蒸馏在多智能体交互环境中因学生rollout偏离参考轨迹而导致的状态-参考不匹配问题，在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特权on-policy蒸馏通过同步教师利用训练专用参考（如成功轨迹）在每个回合对学生响应重新评分，提供密集监督。但在交互式环境中，学生的前置动作不断改变执行状态，当其采取不同动作或以不同顺序完成子目标时，rollout可能到达参考轨迹未覆盖的状态，使参考对该实际到达状态的指导变得不可靠，导致状态-参考不匹配。因此核心目标是为学生实际执行状态提供与之一致的特权参考指导。

Method: SMRC-SD包含两个关键组件：1）状态匹配路由（State-Matched Routing）：在每个回合验证学生当前执行状态是否与参考轨迹上的某个受支持状态匹配，仅在匹配状态下进行蒸馏，过滤掉参考缺乏局部兼容指导的回合；2）上下文自蒸馏（Contextualized Self-Distillation）：对每个匹配状态，从成功轨迹中构建状态条件化的教师上下文，让监督建立在学生实际到达的状态之上。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，SMRC-SD一致优于无条件的成功完整路径蒸馏。使用Qwen3-1.7B时，ALFWorld任务成功率从0.746提升至0.865，WebShop从0.574提升至0.693。控制实验中的路由和上下文消融表明，选择局部支持的回合与构建状态兼容的教师上下文均是性能提升的来源。

Conclusion: SMRC-SD通过显式确定特权轨迹应在何时（状态匹配路由）以及如何（状态条件化教师上下文）指导学生，有效解决了交互环境中特权蒸馏的状态-参考不匹配问题，在多个基准上验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Privileged on-policy distillation provides dense supervision for multi-turn agents by allowing a synchronized teacher to re-score the student's response at every turn with access to training-only references, such as successful trajectories. In interactive environments, however, the student's preceding actions continually change the execution state. As the student takes different actions or completes subgoals in a different order, its rollout may reach states not covered by the reference, making the reference an unreliable source of guidance for the state actually reached. Applying privileged distillation indiscriminately therefore creates state--reference mismatch. This mismatch motivates a central objective: providing privileged reference guidance that remains compatible with the student's current execution state. We introduce State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation (SMRC-SD), which explicitly determines when and how a privileged trajectory should guide an on-policy student. At each turn, SMRC-SD verifies whether the student's current execution state matches a supported state along the reference trajectory. Distillation is applied only at matched states, filtering out turns for which the reference lacks locally compatible guidance. For each matched state, SMRC-SD further constructs state-conditioned teacher context from the successful trajectory, grounding supervision in the state actually reached. Across ALFWorld and WebShop, SMRC-SD consistently outperforms unconditional successful full-path distillation. With Qwen3-1.7B, it improves task success from $0.746$ to $0.865$ on ALFWorld and from $0.574$ to $0.693$ on WebShop. Controlled routing and context ablations support both selecting locally supported turns and constructing state-compatible teacher context as contributors to these gains. Code is available at https://github.com/liujunzhuo/SMRC-SD.

</details>


### [190] [Small Foundation Models of Human Cognition and Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05224)
*Nick Oh,Fernand Gobet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究训练了从135M到14B参数规模的14个模型（覆盖四种架构），在Psych-101数据集（160个实验、1070万条试次级选择数据）上进行认知微调。结果显示分布内性能几乎不随规模变化（0.6B-1B参数即可匹配70B基线），但分布外泛化呈现明显更陡峭的规模梯度。通过剥离提示通道和置换试次顺序的诊断实验，发现刺激和反馈内容承载了75.7%的学习信息，剔除此类内容后模型性能低于随机水平。小型认知微调模型有潜力作为心理学实验的噪声上限估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型经人类行为数据微调后被视为通用认知代理，但存在两个未解问题：所需模型规模有多大，以及这些模型是基于任务结构处理还是利用统计捷径。作者旨在系统性地探究模型规模、架构对认知代理性能的影响，并诊断模型实际依赖的信息来源。

Method: 作者在Psych-101数据集（1070万条来自160个实验的试次级选择）上训练了14个从135M到14B参数的模型，覆盖四种架构族。评估分为分布内（保持可信被试）和分布外（泛化到新任务结构）两种场景。此外，设计了两个诊断：在27个实验中逐步剥离四种提示通道（任务指令、实验刺激、结果反馈、选择历史），并置换试次顺序以测试对时间结构的敏感性。

Result: 分布内性能中模型规模影响甚微，所有模型集中在狭窄性能带内，0.6B到1B参数即可匹配70B基线水平。分布外场景中性能带显著展开，更大模型在泛化到新颖任务结构时具有明显优势。诊断实验显示，屏蔽刺激和反馈内容导致75.7%的学习信息丢失且性能降至随机水平以下，证实选择历史不能单独解释模型表现。试次置换测试表明模型对独立试次任务具有置换不变性，但对由先前反应决定试次顺序的任务表现出敏感性。

Conclusion: 小型认知微调模型在分布内任务上表现优异且规模效率高（0.6B-1B即可），但在分布外泛化中规模优势明显。模型主要依赖刺激和反馈内容而非选择历史，具备真正的任务结构处理能力。这类小型模型可作为心理学实验的噪声上限估计器，但其适用范围仍受制于训练中见过的范式。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned on human behavioural data have emerged as general-purpose cognitive proxies, but the scale this requires, and whether these models process task structure or exploit statistical shortcuts, remain open questions. We train fourteen models from 135M to 14B parameters across four architecture families on Psych-101, a dataset of 10.7 million trial-level choices from 160 experiments. In-distribution, scale barely matters. The models fall within a narrow band, as though against a ceiling, and 0.6B to 1B parameters suffice to match a 70B baseline on held-out participants. Out-of-distribution, that band opens into a markedly steeper scaling gradient, with larger models clearly advantaged in generalisation to novel task structure. To determine what information these models use, we run two diagnostics. We progressively strip four prompt channels -- task instructions, experimental stimuli, outcome feedback, and choice history -- across 27 experiments, and permute trial order. Masking the content of stimuli and feedback destroys 75.7% of learned information and pushes models below chance, demonstrating that choice history alone does not account for performance. Permutation reveals invariance on tasks with independent trials but sensitivity where trial order is determined by prior responses. Small cognitively fine-tuned models therefore show promise as noise ceiling estimators for psychological experiments, though their scope remains bounded by the paradigms seen in training.

</details>


### [191] [OPERA: Operator-residual feedback for reliable autonomous optical experiments with language-model agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05990)
*Ning Xu,Xiang Zheng,Fuqiang Zhong,Huadong Wang,Xiaolong Wu,Zhiyuan Liu,Hui Ning*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OPERA框架，一种用于光学实验的算子-残差框架。它将实验动作表示为光学算子，并用物理可解释的残差评估其结果，引导自主代理选择、组合或生成操作算子。在三个光学任务中，相比仅用分数反馈，算子-残差反馈将"分数提升但无物理改善"的决策比例从23.6-39.0%降至0.9-1.9%，同时提升了任务达成率并降低了实验预算，数字孪生中选择的方案已成功迁移至三台光学仪器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主代理通常基于可能无法反映实验真实成功的分数来选择动作，导致分数提升但物理上并无改进的决策频繁出现，缺乏物理可解释的反馈信号来指导实验自主决策。

Method: 提出OPERA（Operator-Residual框架）：将实验动作编码为光学算子（可执行的对测量、控制或重建的变更），用残差（对指定物理条件的偏离度）评估算子执行结果。代理同时利用算子和残差来选算子、组合算子或生成新算子，物理性能通过留出参考独立评估。

Result: 在三个光学任务中，仅分数反馈时23.6-39.0%的决策出现分数提升但无物理改善，而算子-残差反馈仅0.9-1.9%。算子-残差反馈提高了达成并维持任务目标的概率，降低了实验预算；数字孪生方案迁移至三台光学仪器，结构光重建中重复实验显示投影预算降低。

Conclusion: 算子和残差共同提供了可测量的物理证据来引导自主决策，使得自主代理的决策建立在物理可解释的基础上，优于纯分数驱动的反馈机制。

Abstract: Autonomous agents choose actions using scores that may not reflect experimental success. We developed OPERA, an operator-residual framework for optical experiments. It represents experimental actions as optical operators and evaluates their outcomes using physically interpretable residuals. Operators specify executable changes to measurement, control or reconstruction, while residuals report departures from specified physical conditions. The agent uses both to select, combine or generate operators, and physical performance is evaluated independently against a withheld reference. Across three optical tasks, score-only feedback produced score increases without physical improvement in 23.6--39.0\% of decisions, compared with 0.9--1.9\% for operator-residual feedback. Operator-residual feedback increased the probability of reaching and maintaining task targets and reduced experimental budgets. Protocols selected in digital twins were transferred to three optical instruments, and repeated experiments showed a lower projection budget in structured-light reconstruction. Together, operators and residuals guide autonomous decisions using measurable physical evidence.

</details>


### [192] [Project2Task: Graph-Guided Project-Level Planning for Autonomous Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05225)
*Huirui Xu,Runtao Xu,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Project2Task，一个基于图引导的项目级规划层，将研究项目（project brief）分解为有向谱系图中的"创新原子"（innovation atoms），并通过轻量级Bernoulli块模型目标在水平、垂直和混合组合分解中做出选择，生成具有明确贡献归属、依赖感知的任务契约（task contracts），从而实现从项目到可执行任务的系统化规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究代理系统能将单个话题驱动为文献检索、假设提出、代码生成、实验运行和稿件撰写，但研究项目并非单一大型任务，而是需要通过多个有界任务推进的长期议程。现有单任务系统将项目视为一个超大任务，产生模糊或重叠的任务集合，或需人工协调任务边界和执行顺序，缺乏项目级的结构化规划能力。

Method: 1) 将候选贡献表示为"创新原子"（innovation atoms），并在有向谱系图（directed lineage graph）中组织它们；2) 使用轻量级Bernoulli块模型目标在水平、垂直和混合组合分解之间进行选择；3) 生成具有明确贡献归属的有界任务，修复重叠和缺失的执行字段；4) 输出依赖感知的任务契约，规范目标、输入、预期产物、评估要求、边界约束、依赖关系和执行顺序。契约与具体下游研究执行器无关，可集成任务输出为连贯的项目级结果。

Result: 在包含10个项目简报、约30个任务的基准测试上：基于稿件的组合评估中，Project2Task平均质量得分7.15，优于Brief Baseline（4.58）和Topic-only Setting（5.31）；与AutoResearchClaw集成后，下游任务平均准确率从0.536提升至0.759。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，显式的项目到任务规划对于生成连贯、非冗余且可执行的研究任务组合具有重要价值，验证了Project2Task在自主研究项目规划层面的有效性。

Abstract: Research agents can increasingly search literature, propose hypotheses, generate code, run experiments, and draft manuscripts from a single topic. However, a research project is not merely a larger task: it is a long-horizon agenda that must be advanced through multiple bounded tasks with distinct but related objectives, parallel alternatives, and dependency-aware sequences. Existing single-task systems often treat the project as one oversized task, produce a flat set of vague or overlapping tasks, or leave task boundaries and execution order to manual coordination. We introduce Project2Task, a graph-guided project-level planning layer for autonomous research. Given a project brief, it represents candidate contributions as innovation atoms and organizes them in a directed lineage graph. A lightweight Bernoulli block-model objective selects among horizontal, vertical, and hybrid portfolio decompositions. Project2Task then generates bounded tasks with explicit contribution ownership, repairs overlaps and missing execution fields, and emits dependency-aware task contracts that specify objectives, inputs, expected artifacts, evaluation requirements, boundary constraints, dependencies, and execution order. The contracts are independent of any particular downstream research executor and support integration of task outputs into a coherent project-level result. On a benchmark of ten project briefs yielding roughly 30 tasks, manuscript-based portfolio evaluation gives Project2Task an average quality score of 7.15, compared with 4.58 for the Brief Baseline and 5.31 for the Topic-only Setting. Integrating its contracts with AutoResearchClaw increases average downstream task accuracy from 0.536 to 0.759. These results demonstrate the value of explicit project-to-task planning for producing coherent, non-redundant, and executable research-task portfolios.

</details>


### [193] [TriQua: Reconciling Granularity and Context in Factuality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05228)
*Jin Liu,Steffen Thoma,Achim Rettinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TriQua框架，通过按复杂度灵活建模事实（简单声明用标准三元组，复杂声明用附加上下文限定符的超关系事实），解决了LLM事实性评估中"先分解后验证"范式的原子性与上下文保留之间的权衡，并配套提出TriQuaScore量化事实性评分。实验证明TriQuaScore与人类标注高度一致，TriQua分解质量稳健且优于现有基于分解的事实验证框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的"先分解后验证"范式存在根本性权衡：原子事实（一句话表达单一信息单元）往往丢失必要的上下文，而更宽泛的声明又缺乏进行精确评估所需的粒度。需要一个灵活的事实建模框架来解决这一矛盾。

Method: TriQua框架包含两个核心组件：1) 自适应事实分解——简单声明提取为标准三元组，复杂声明表示为附加辅助上下文限定符的超关系事实；2) TriQuaScore——用于量化这些结构化事实单元的事实性评分方法。验证过程直接在特定三元组和限定符上标注具体错误，提供细粒度的可解释性。

Result: TriQuaScore与人类标注的事实性评分高度一致；TriQua实现了稳健的分解质量，并且在基于证据的事实验证任务上优于现有的基于分解的框架。

Conclusion: 通过灵活的自适应事实结构，TriQua在保留必要上下文的同时不牺牲原子性，实现了精准的事实验证和细粒度的错误可解释性，为LLM事实性评估提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: The "decompose-then-verify" paradigm for LLM factuality evaluation faces a fundamental trade-off: atomic facts, i.e., one sentence conveying one unit of information, often omit essential context, while broader statements lack the granularity needed for precise assessment. To address this, we introduce TriQua, a framework that flexibly models facts based on their complexity. Simple claims are extracted as standard triples, while complex claims are represented as hyperrelational facts by attaching auxiliary contextual qualifiers. This adaptive structure preserves the necessary context for accurate retrieval and verification without sacrificing atomicity. Furthermore, TriQua's verification process directly annotates concrete errors within specific triples and qualifiers, providing fine-grained explainability for error detection. Alongside the framework, we propose TriQuaScore to quantify the factuality of these structured fact units. Empirical evaluations show that TriQuaScore strongly aligns with human annotated factuality scores, TriQua achieves robust decomposition quality, and outperforms existing decomposition-based frameworks in evidence-based fact verification.

</details>


### [194] [Coherence-Oriented Dream Scene Visualisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05233)
*Azra Açıl,Simon Colton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV)系统，可将梦境文字描述转化为四帧时序图像序列，利用大语言模型将梦境分为四个时间片段，再由文生图模型生成视觉连贯的图像序列，并通过CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL等模型进行客观评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梦境往往情感强烈但难以用语言准确传达，现有方法缺乏将梦境文字描述转化为多帧时序视觉叙事的能力，需要一种能够捕捉梦境情感体验并可视化展示的系统。

Method: DSV系统采用两阶段流水线：首先使用大语言模型将梦境描述分割为四个时间顺序的部分；然后使用文本到图像生成模型为每个部分生成图像，并在序列间保持视觉连贯性；对于与文本不匹配的图像，DSV会重新生成。

Result: 在DreamBank的梦境描述基础上评估了50个可视化结果，使用CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL视觉语言模型作为目标度量，报告了质量、保真度和连贯性指标。

Conclusion: DSV成功实现了将梦境文字描述转化为时序视觉叙事的自动化流程，并通过多模态模型提供了质量、保真度和连贯性的客观评估框架。

Abstract: Dreams can be emotionally intense but difficult to communicate. We describe the Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV) system which turns written dream descriptions into a temporal sequence of four panel images visualising the dream. This starts with a large language model prompted to split a dream description into four chronological parts. Then a text-to-image model produces images for each part with visual coherence maintained across the sequence, and DSV regenerates any image not suitably matching the text. We evaluate DSV over 50 visualisations from dream descriptions in DreamBank, and report quality, fidelity and coherence results via objective measures employing the CLIP, DINOv2 and Qwen2-VL vision-language models.

</details>


### [195] [Search2Skill: Skill Distillation Beyond Knowledge Boundaries Via Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05245)
*Muyang Ye,Tian Lan,Feihu Jiang,Yongshi Ye,Wuyunsiqin,Bin Zhu,Qianghuai Jia,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Ye Wang,Jinyang Zhang,Longyue Wang,Lingfeng Bao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Search2Skill框架，通过强化学习自动识别智能体能力缺口、从外部搜索证据并将其蒸馏为可复用技能，在八个专家级领域的三个基准上优于搜索增强和基于轨迹的技能学习基线，且技能具有跨模型规模迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化技能方法仅从模型参数化知识或轨迹中内部构建技能，受限于模型已有的知识边界。然而，专业任务背后的领域约定和标准流程往往超出该边界，难以仅靠智能体自身引出，导致技能自进化在专家领域存在根本瓶颈。

Method: 提出Search2Skill框架，包含三个环节：自动识别智能体的能力缺口、搜索外部来源以弥补缺口、将检索证据蒸馏为结构化的可复用技能。整个框架通过基于评分标准（rubric）的强化学习方案进行联合优化，同时改进何时搜索、如何搜索以及如何生成技能三个决策环节。

Result: 在来自三个基准的八个专家级领域上，Search2Skill在流式评估和留出评估两种协议下均一致优于搜索增强基线和基于轨迹的技能学习基线。进一步分析表明，性能提升源于技能抽象而非原始检索证据，且学到的技能可跨模型规模迁移。

Conclusion: Search2Skill通过引入外部知识源打破了模型参数化知识的边界限制，使LLM智能体能够在专家领域实现真正的自我进化，且技能抽象是性能提升的关键因素，技能具备可迁移性。

Abstract: Reusable skills, which encapsulate the procedural knowledge required to solve real-world professional tasks, offer LLM-based agents a path toward self-evolution in expert domains. Existing self-evolving skill methods construct skills internally from the model's parametric knowledge or trajectories, and are therefore bounded by what the model already knows. However, the domain conventions and standard procedures underlying professional skills often lie beyond this boundary and are hard to elicit from the agent alone. To address this issue, we therefore propose a novel framework, Search2Skill, that automatically identifies the agent's capability gaps, searches external sources to address them, and distills the retrieved evidence into structured, reusable skills. Specifically, Search2Skill is optimized by a rubric-based reinforcement learning scheme that jointly improves when to search, how to search, and how to generate skills. Experiments on eight expert-level domains from three benchmarks show that Search2Skill consistently outperforms both search-augmented and trajectory-based skill-learning baselines under both streaming and held-out evaluation protocols. Further analyses show that the gains arise from skill abstraction rather than raw retrieved evidence, and that the acquired skills transfer across model scales.

</details>


### [196] [LUNAR: Benchmarking Personalized Large Language Models on UNiversal User BehAvioR Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05246)
*Jiahao Zhang,Yongzhi Tong,Zelin Fu,Pengde Zhao,Yanmei Jiang,Jiang Feng,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LUNAR是首个基于纵向应用交互历史评估LLM跨领域行为个性化的基准，通过多阶段合成流程构建，覆盖衣食住行四大日常领域。实验表明行为日志对个性化是必要但不充分的，证据选择、跨领域整合和隐私控制是关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化LLM基准主要依赖文本人物画像或孤立行为信号，缺乏对跨领域行为个性化的评估——即回复需要基于异构的日常活动（衣食住行）进行个性化生成。这一缺口限制了该方向的研究进展。

Method: 提出LUNAR基准，其核心贡献在于：1) 构建基于纵向应用交互历史的多领域个性化评估框架；2) 设计多阶段从粗到细的合成流程（coarse-to-fine synthesis pipeline），基于真实行为模式合成数据，以应对数据稀疏和隐私问题；3) 通过保真度分析验证合成数据与真实行为分布的一致性。

Result: 在19个主流LLM上的实验发现：行为日志访问对个性化是必要但不充分的；更多上下文或更大模型并不能保证更好的个性化表现；有效个性化依赖于跨领域的证据选择和整合。细粒度行为记录的直接检索稳定优于压缩记忆，但更强的个性化可能以牺牲隐私保护为代价。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了个人化LLM面临的三大核心挑战：证据选择、跨领域信息整合和隐私控制。这些发现为该领域的后续研究提供了明确的方向指引。

Abstract: Existing personalized LLM benchmarks primarily rely on textual personas or isolated behavioral signals, providing limited evaluation of cross-domain behavioral personalization, where responses must be grounded in heterogeneous daily-life activities. To address this gap, we introduce LUNAR, the first benchmark for evaluating how LLMs personalize responses from longitudinal app interaction histories across universal daily-life domains, including clothing, food, housing, and mobility. To support scalable benchmark construction while mitigating data sparsity and privacy concerns, LUNAR uses a multi-stage coarse-to-fine synthesis pipeline grounded in real-world behavioral patterns. Fidelity analyses show closer alignment with real behavioral distributions than other synthetic benchmarks. Experiments on 19 mainstream LLMs show that access to behavioral logs is necessary but not sufficient for deep personalization: neither more context nor larger models guarantees better performance; effective personalization depends on selecting and integrating relevant evidence across domains. Direct retrieval of fine-grained behavioral records consistently outperforms compressed memory, while stronger personalization can come at the cost of privacy protection. These findings identify evidence selection, cross-domain integration, and privacy control as key challenges for personalized LLMs.

</details>


### [197] [WorldClaw: Agentic 3D Open-World Generation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05248)
*Chunchao Guo,Jinpeng Li,Yang Li,Zilong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldClaw是一个完全智能体驱动的粗到细框架，用于从开放式文本提示生成大规模、可自由探索的3D开放世界场景，同时保持全局空间连贯性和丰富的局部内容，并产出可编辑的实例级资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从开放式文本生成大规模、可自由探索的3D世界面临重大挑战：系统需要同时维护全局空间连贯性、丰富的局部内容，以及适合下游编辑和复用的显式资源。现有方法难以兼顾这三方面的要求。

Method: WorldClaw采用完全智能体化（agentic）的粗到细（coarse-to-fine）框架：首先由规划智能体将文本提示转化为包含区域、地形、资源、材质和空间关系的结构化规范；然后构建全局连贯的地形基础（基于语义布局、可复用资源、生成/程序化材质及区域感知高度场）；对于细节要求高的区域，生成地形条件的合成场景，重建可编辑的纹理网格，并恢复其在地形上的位置；最后通过基于渲染的智能体进一步细化地形、物体、外观和接触关系。

Result: 在多种开放式世界提示下，WorldClaw生成了具有连贯空间组织、视觉吸引力局部内容和可编辑实例级资源的大规模场景，同时保持了全局一致的地形结构。

Conclusion: WorldClaw通过分层智能体协作和粗到细的生成策略，有效解决了开放世界3D场景生成中全局一致性、局部丰富性与资源可编辑性之间的权衡，为大规模开放3D世界生成提供了一个完整可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Generating large-scale, freely explorable 3D worlds from open-ended text remains challenging because a system must jointly maintain global spatial coherence, rich local content, and explicit assets suitable for downstream editing and reuse. We present WorldClaw, a fully agentic, coarse-to-fine framework for open-world 3D scene generation. Planning agents translate a text prompt into a structured specification of regions, terrain, assets, materials, and spatial relations. WorldClaw then builds a globally coherent terrain foundation from semantic layouts, reusable assets, generative or procedural materials, and a region-aware height field. For detail-demanding regions, it generates terrain-conditioned compositions, reconstructs editable textured meshes, and recovers their placement on the terrain; render-based agents further refine terrain, objects, appearance, and contacts. Across diverse open-world prompts, WorldClaw produces large-scale scenes with coherent spatial organization, visually compelling local content, and editable instance-level assets while preserving a consistent global terrain structure.

</details>


### [198] [Posture and Sustainment Optimization Under Adversarial Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05256)
*Amelie Norris,Alyssa Lee,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于联合行动规划中"预先承诺态势"（即冲突情境未解决前将军事资产预先部署到位）问题的场景加权对抗鲁棒态势优化引擎，包含CEV优化器和其对抗性扩展RobustCEV，通过三个实验证明其相对于贪心基线的显著效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预先承诺态势（Pre-commitment posture）是联合作战规划中一个关键但尚无正式解决方案的问题。当前实践依赖贪心启发式算法，仅最大化价值而忽略地理覆盖，且对针对高战略价值地点的对手存在结构性脆弱性。因此需要一种形式化的优化方法来综合考虑地理覆盖、威胁场景和对抗性行为。

Method: 将态势与可持续性分配（PSA）问题建模为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），涵盖资产、战区位置和时间步三个维度。提出两个核心方法：（1）CEV（Composite Expected Value）优化器，通过最大化场景加权的预期态势效率来部署资产，覆盖威胁场景分布；（2）RobustCEV扩展，通过迭代对抗一个贝叶斯对手来优化，该对手会根据观察到的部署方案更新其目标分配分布。

Result: 在包含20个资产和5个战区位置的印太基地环境中进行了三项实验：（1）贪心基线因地理覆盖不足产生25.1%的永久态势效率损失，且在价值相关的对抗威胁下出现57.3%的场景加权战备崩溃；（2）当威胁分布携带地理信号时，CEV优化器比贪心算法最多恢复19.8%的效率，5至20个精选场景足以捕获大部分收益；（3）在自适应对手采用欺骗性威胁先验时，RobustCEV扩展相对朴素优化器最多恢复158%的效率。所有结果均通过带Bonferroni校正的配对t检验和两层方差分解验证。

Conclusion: 性能差距是部署策略的结构性属性而非采样伪影。CEV和RobustCEV在场景加权态势效率、对抗鲁棒性和地理覆盖方面系统性优于现有的贪心启发式方法，为联合行动规划中的资产预先部署提供了形式化的求解框架。

Abstract: Pre-commitment posture, the assignment of military assets to theater locations before conflict scenarios resolve, is a critical and formally unsolved problem in joint operational planning. Current practice relies on greedy heuristics that maximize value and ignore geographic coverage and are structurally vulnerable to adversaries that target high-strategic value locations. This paper presents a scenario-weighted adversarially robust posture optimization engine for the Posture and sustainability allocation (PSA) problem, modeled as a finite-horizon Markov Decision Process over assets, theater locations, and time steps. We introduce the Composite Expected Value (CEV) optimizer, which places assets by maximizing scenario-weighted expected posture efficiency over a distribution of threat scenarios, and the RobustCEV extension, which iterates against a Bayesian adversary that updates its targeting distribution in response to observed placement. Across three experiments in an Indo-Pacific basing environment with 20 assets and 5 theater locations, we demonstrate that: (1) the greedy baseline incurs a permanent 25.1% posture efficiency penalty due to geographic under-coverage and a 57.3% scenario-weighted readiness collapse under value-correlated adversarial threat; (2) the CEV optimizer recovers up to 19.8% efficiency over greedy when the threat distribution carries a geographic signal, with a curated set of 5 to 20 scenarios sufficient to capture the majority of this gain; and (3) the RobustCEV extension recovers up to 158% efficiency relative to a naive optimizer when an adaptive adversary employs a deceptive threat prior. All findings are validated using paired t-tests with Bonferroni correction and two-level variance decomposition, confirming that the performance gaps reported are structural properties of placement strategies rather than sampling artifacts.

</details>


### [199] [OrchestraBench: Evaluating Multi-Agent Orchestration Failure Modes, Recovery, and Decomposition Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05263)
*Yidian Chen,Yingzi Gu,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty,Sharon Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OrchestraBench是一个针对多智能体编排框架的故障注入与诊断基准，通过种子可复现的故障注入机制和级联半径、按故障模式恢复率等新指标，系统地评估了路由策略和恢复机制在不同流水线深度下的失效模式、级联传播与恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常只报告任务准确率，无法诊断流水线为何失败、级联从何处开始、哪个路由决策导致崩溃。随着多智能体编排框架从demo走向生产，需要一个能系统评估故障、恢复和解耦能力的诊断性基准。

Method: 通过受控、种子可复现的故障注入机制，在模板化企业工作流上构建测试环境；引入级联半径（cascade radius）和按故障模式恢复率（per-failure-mode recovery）作为主要指标；使用带bootstrap置信区间和配对检验比较路由策略；设计26例金标诊断集和受控机制探针（含Claude agent和可验证的算术依赖链）。

Result: 关键词/标志路由在对具有误导性或缺失表面标志的对抗性案例上得分为0%，而意图推理模型路由得分为100%（与oracle一致）。在五类MAST模式中发现三个故障处理层级：工具故障完全恢复(1.0)、模糊委托部分恢复(0.30)、三种潜在或语义故障从不恢复(0.0)。该排序在贷款审批工作流以及Sonnet/Opus/Haiku模型间保持一致。盲重试会复现潜在故障并增加检测时间；级联半径随流水线深度增加（深度3-7时均值从0.9到4.7）；可信状态修复消融显示表现为恢复的收益主要来自可信状态信号而非自主检测。

Conclusion: OrchestraBench提供了可控的故障注入诊断框架，揭示了多智能体编排中检测与归因对遏制级联故障的必要性，同时强调其结果是受控链机制探针而非领域工作负载声明。

Abstract: Multi-agent orchestration frameworks are moving from demos to production, yet benchmarks typically report task accuracy without diagnosing why a pipeline failed, where a cascade began, or which routing decision caused the breakdown. OrchestraBench evaluates failure, recovery, and decomposition through a controlled, seed-reproducible failure-injection harness over templated enterprise workflows. It introduces cascade radius and per-failure-mode recovery as primary metrics and compares routing policies with bootstrap confidence intervals and paired tests. On a 26-case gold-labelled diagnostic, a keyword/flag router scored 0% on adversarial cases with misleading or missing surface flags, whereas an intent-reasoning model router scored 100%, matching the oracle. Controlled mechanism probes with a real Claude agent over a verifiable arithmetic dependency chain revealed three failure-handling tiers across five MAST modes: tool faults recovered fully (1.0), ambiguous delegation recovered partially (0.30), and three latent or semantic modes never recovered (0.0). This ordering persisted when the computation was reframed as a loan-approval workflow and across Sonnet, Opus, and Haiku, although absolute rates shifted with context. Blind retry reproduced latent faults and increased time to detection, indicating that detection and attribution are necessary for containment. Cascade radius increased with pipeline depth (mean 0.9 to 4.7 across depths 3-7). A trusted-state repair ablation showed that apparent containment gains primarily came from the trusted-state signal rather than autonomous detection. These results are controlled-chain mechanism probes, not domain-workload claims.

</details>


### [200] [Agentic self-driving microscopy benchmarks support qualification but do not necessarily generalize to unseen tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05266)
*Nathan S Johnson,Ian Abshire*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基准测试和追踪日志框架，用于评估不同多智能体架构在显微镜控制任务中的表现。通过53项显微镜基准测试、105种智能体配置和1949次运行，发现架构选择对延迟、令牌使用、成本和失败模式有显著影响，但基于架构和测试结果训练的替代模型无法可靠预测智能体在新任务上的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体正被越来越多地用于控制各类科学表征工具（如显微镜和同步辐射光束线），但针对物理基础设施的智能体控制研究尚处于早期阶段，缺乏成熟的工程范式。研究人员在设计显微镜智能体时面临大量架构决策（LLM选择、智能体数量、职责分配、RAG参数等），不仅需要确保智能体能正确执行已知任务，还希望其能泛化到未见过的新任务。因此需要一个系统性的基准框架来揭示不同架构选择对任务表现的影响，以及现有基准在预测泛化能力方面的局限性。

Method: 本研究开发了一个基准测试和追踪日志框架，用于评估一智能体、二智能体和三智能体的图拓扑结构、五种LLM、RAG与上下文参数以及操作约束。框架记录了53项显微镜基准测试，共涵盖105种智能体配置、1949次独立测试运行和49109条RAG检索。通过直接比较不同配置在延迟、令牌使用、成本和失败模式上的差异，并使用在测试结果上训练的替代模型来预测智能体对未见任务的泛化表现。

Result: 直接比较显示不同配置在延迟、令牌使用、成本和失败模式之间存在显著差异。然而，基于智能体架构和测试结果训练的替代模型不能可靠预测智能体在新任务上的表现。这表明当前异构测试套件在资格认证、回归测试、诊断和直接比较方面是有用的，但无法支持任务无关的全局配置模型。

Conclusion: 现有的显微镜智能体基准测试适用于限定条件内的配置评估、回归测试、故障诊断和直接对比，但当前异构测试套件无法支撑建立一个任务无关的全局最优配置模型。这意味着研究人员在部署智能体到新任务时，不能依赖全局配置预测，而应通过基准测试进行具体场景下的验证和调优。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly being developed to control a wide range of scientific characterization tools including microscopes and synchrotron beamlines. Research into agentic control of physical infrastructure is nascent and there are few well-established paradigms for how to engineer an agentic system. There are many choices to make when designing a microscopy agent, including the choice of LLM, the number of agents to use, agent responsibilities and delegation rules, retrieval-augmented generation parameters, and more. When designing and optimizing an agentic microscope controller, researchers not only want to ensure that the agent can correctly perform known tasks but also that the agent can generalize to new tasks that it has not encountered before. In this study, we develop a benchmark and trace-logging framework that reveals a) how different choices of agent architecture impact performance at microscopy tasks and b) the limitations of benchmarks for predicting if a particular agent will perform well on unseen microscopy tasks. The framework was used to evaluate one-, two-, and three-agent graph topologies, five LLMs, RAG and context parameters, and operational constraints across 53 microscopy benchmark tests. In total, 105 agent configurations, 1,949 individual test runs, and 49,109 RAG retrievals were recorded. Direct comparisons showed clear differences in latency, token use, cost, and failure mode between configurations. However, surrogate models trained on agent architecture and test results did not reliably predict an agent's performance on new, unseen tasks. These results show that these benchmarks are useful for qualification, regression testing, diagnosis, and direct comparison, but the current heterogeneous test suite does not support a task-independent global configuration model.

</details>


### [201] [CASCADE: An Agentic Regulatory Network Framework for Patient-Data-Validated Downstream Perturbation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05359)
*Jose A. Bird*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍CASCADE，一个通过MCP接口从预计算的ARACNe调控网络中预测基因扰动下游转录效应的智能体框架。与以往仅验证预测基因是否为已知癌症基因的做法不同，作者利用TCGA病人肿瘤数据中焦点基因拷贝数扩增作为敲低的剂量代理，检验预测方向是否与真实表达变化一致。MYC预测效果良好（三种癌症亚型准确率72-90%），但扩展到15个额外基因后发现验证是基因特异性的：增殖机制调控因子大多有效，而谱系身份转录因子和CCND2持续失败。此外，对LLM智能体将自然语言请求映射到真实MCP工具调用的能力进行基准测试，小型模型精确匹配率71.4%，较大模型85.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基因扰动预测工具的验证方法存在根本缺陷：仅仅检查预测的基因是否属于已知癌症基因（成员资格），而不验证预测的方向（上调或下调）是否符合真实生物学。本文旨在建立一个更严格的验证框架，同时评估智能体本身在自然语言请求与工具调用映射上的可靠性。

Method: 利用TCGA真实病人肿瘤数据中焦点基因拷贝数扩增作为基因敲低的逆方向剂量代理：扩增样本的基因表达与敲低效应相反，因此可与预测的敲低靶标方向进行对照验证。针对MYC在BRCA、COAD、STAD三个癌症类型中验证，用置换检验建立基线，用PAM50亚型作为对照控制混杂因素，并在METABRIC独立队列中复现。使用Fisher精确检验与MSigDB基因集基线比较。扩展到15个额外基因检查验证的普适性。同时对LLM智能体进行精确匹配率基准测试，评估其将自然语言请求正确映射到MCP工具调用的能力。

Result: MYC预测的敲低靶标与真实扩增vs非扩增肿瘤表达高度一致（BRCA 90.0%，COAD 72.0%，STAD 85.7%，均p<0.0013），显著高于置换基线，通过PAM50亚型对照并在METABRIC独立队列复现（87.2%）。Fisher精确检验表明CASCADE准确性未显著超越MSigDB公共知识基线，但基因特异性方向判定优于朴素均匀猜测。扩展到15个额外基因：增殖机制调控因子大多有效，谱系身份转录因子和CCND2失败。LLM基准测试：本地模型精确匹配率71.4%，较大模型85.7%；两模型在含糊查询时均自信地默认错误的扰动类型，且修复失败因为触发条件从未出现。

Conclusion: CASCADE提出的方向性验证框架比传统成员资格验证更严格且具有实际意义，但扩展验证揭示了基因特异性的异质性——工具在增殖机制相关基因上表现良好，在谱系身份基因上失败。与现有公共知识基线相比未见显著优势，表明该框架的价值更多在于独立验证而非超越现有知识。LLM智能体在工具调用映射上存在可识别的失败模式，特别是含糊查询下的错误默认行为难以通过针对性修复解决。

Abstract: CASCADE is an agentic framework that predicts downstream transcriptional effects of gene perturbation from precomputed
  ARACNe regulatory networks, exposed via MCP. Prior work validates such tools by checking whether predicted genes are
  known cancer genes (membership); we instead test whether the predicted direction of change matches reality, using
  focal-gene copy-number amplification as a dosage-based proxy for the inverse of knockdown against real TCGA patient
  tumor data.
  For MYC, CASCADE's predicted knockdown targets show strong concordance with real amplified-vs-non-amplified tumor
  expression across three cancer types (BRCA: 90.0%, COAD: 72.0%, STAD: 85.7%; all p<0.0013), well above permutation
  baselines, surviving a PAM50 subtype control and replicating in an independent cohort (METABRIC, 87.2%). Compared
  against curated MSigDB gene-set baselines via Fisher's exact test, CASCADE's accuracy is not shown to exceed existing
  public knowledge of MYC- or E2F-driven biology, though its gene-specific direction-calling clearly outperforms a naive
  uniform guess.
  Extending to fifteen additional genes, validation proves gene-specific rather than universal: proliferation-machinery
  regulators mostly replicate, while lineage-identity transcription factors and one cyclin-D paralog (CCND2)
  consistently fail, a pattern we discuss as a hedged, post-hoc hypothesis.
  We separately benchmark whether an LLM-based agent correctly grounds natural-language requests into CASCADE's real MCP
  tool calls. Across 35 queries, a documented local model reaches 71.4% exact match (85.7% for a larger model); schema
  and gene-alias failures are resolved by scale or server-side correction, but both models confidently default to the
  wrong perturbation type on ambiguous queries, a failure a targeted fix could not resolve because its trigger condition
  never occurs.

</details>


### [202] [Counterfactual Analysis via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05367)
*Zonghao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了GPT-3.5在反事实分析中的应用，聚焦于在线借贷场景，通过提示工程将GPT的预测R²从1.97%提升至2.84%，接近梯度提升回归的3.48%，并展示了GPT在生成反事实ROI时的逻辑一致性与因果推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实分析对决策至关重要，但传统方法在复杂场景下存在局限。作者希望探索大规模语言模型（LLM）是否能够有效用于反事实分析，特别是在在线借贷中评估不同利率方案的投资回报率（ROI），以拓展LLM在预测和决策领域的应用边界。

Method: 作者以GPT-3.5为研究对象，首先对比GPT与传统机器学习算法（如梯度提升回归）在ROI预测上的表现，并利用提示工程优化GPT的预测能力；随后使用GPT生成多个替代利率方案下的反事实ROI，检验其逻辑连贯性和因果推理能力。

Result: 提示工程使GPT的R²从1.97%提升至2.84%，逼近梯度提升回归的3.48%；GPT在生成反事实ROI时表现出良好的逻辑一致性和因果推理能力，验证了LLM在反事实分析中的潜力。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型有潜力成为在线借贷及其他领域反事实分析的有效工具，为预测和决策任务提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Counterfactual analysis aims to predict potential outcomes under hypothetical scenarios, offering valuable insights for decision-making. This paper investigates the application of large language models (LLMs), specifically the GPT-3.5 model, for counterfactual analysis. We focus on the online lending context, where the counterfactual return on investment (ROI) is crucial for evaluating different interest rate schemes. We begin by assessing the predictive performance of GPT and comparing it with advanced machine learning algorithms. The results show that prompt engineering can significantly enhance GPT's predictions, with the R-squared increasing from 1.97% to 2.84%, closely approaching the 3.48% achieved by gradient-boosted regression. Subsequently, we utilize GPT to generate counterfactual ROIs under a set of alternative interest rates. GPT exhibits logical coherence and causal reasoning in its responses. The findings underscore the potential of LLMs as effective tools for counterfactual analysis in online lending, suggesting broader applications for LLMs in various predictive and decision-making contexts.

</details>


### [203] [DoctorAgents: an agentic framework to iteratively refine AutoML pipeline for small clinical temporal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05375)
*Ruilin Wang,Bo-Hong Wang,Elizabeth Kourbatski,Jun Bai,Hegang Chen,Ziyang Song,Gilles Boire,Marie Hudson,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DoctorAgents，一个基于大语言模型智能体的AutoML框架，通过将小型临床数据的AutoML从穷举搜索转变为推理驱动的精化，利用专门的生成、验证和精化智能体协作，并通过文本梯度下降反向传播自然语言反馈，实现无需穷举搜索的端到端机器学习流水线自动构建与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床机器学习虽有潜力支持高风险医疗决策，但数据的稀缺性、异质性和时间复杂性制约了其可靠部署。现有AutoML系统主要依赖在预定义空间内进行暴力搜索，缺乏显式推理和记忆能力，无法有效应对小型临床数据，因此需要将AutoML范式从穷举搜索转变为推理驱动的精化。

Method: DoctorAgents由三类专门的LLM智能体组成：生成智能体（generation）、验证智能体（validation）和精化智能体（refinement），它们协同工作以端到端地构建和优化ML流水线。核心机制是通过文本梯度下降（textual gradient descent）将自然语言反馈反向传播，实现对流水线的定向更新，而非穷举搜索。

Result: 在多个不同的临床任务上，DoctorAgents持续优于已有的AutoML基线方法，同时能够生成更具可解释性的任务特定特征表示。

Conclusion: DoctorAgents验证了将AutoML从穷举搜索重构为基于推理和自然语言反馈精化的可行性，为小型临床数据上的机器学习流水线自动化提供了更高效、可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Clinical machine learning (ML) has the potential to support high-stakes medical decision-making, but reliable deployment is often constrained by scarce, heterogeneous, and temporal complexity. Developing effective ML pipelines for such data remains time-consuming and error-prone, while existing automated machine learning (AutoML) systems only partially address this challenge because they largely rely on brute-force search over predefined spaces and lack explicit reasoning and memory. We therefore reformulate AutoML for small clinical data from exhaustive search to reasoning-driven refinement. We propose DoctorAgents, an agentic AI framework that autonomously constructs and optimizes end-to-end ML pipelines through specialized large language model (LLM) agents for generation, validation, and refinement. DoctorAgents backpropagates natural-language feedback through textual gradient descent to perform targeted updates without exhaustive search. Experiments across diverse clinical tasks show that DoctorAgents consistently outperforms established AutoML baselines while producing more interpretable task-specific representations.

</details>


### [204] [C$^3$PO: Evaluating Cross-Modal Composition and Counterfactual Performance in Omnimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05381)
*Swapnanil Mukherjee,Agyeya Negi,Tanuja Ganu,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C³PO基准，包含3,404个跨视频、音频、图像和文本的多模态样本，用于评估信息组合和反事实冲突两种推理能力。实验发现人类准确率达88.64%，最佳模型Gemini-3.1-Pro仅达73.17%，且86-95%的失败源于模态主导性问题，模型过早聚焦单一模态而忽略矛盾证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型虽然能处理多种感官输入，但其推理仍严重偏向主导模态，导致跨模态推理脆弱且不可靠。现有基准缺乏对跨模态推理"何时失败以及为何失败"的系统性诊断工具。

Method: 构建C³PO基准，通过25个逻辑模板的自动生成管线创建3,404个样本，采用成对的IC（信息组合）/CC（反事实冲突）结构和四层设计来区分诊断跨模态推理失败的原因。同时使用注意力探针分析模型内部注意力分布，以及中层注意力熵来预测推理正确性。

Result: 人类准确率88.64%，最佳模型Gemini-3.1-Pro仅73.17%，开源模型在冲突场景下表现崩溃。86-95%的失败源于模态主导性，模型将87-95%的注意力集中在文本模态。中层注意力熵可预测准确性：持续探索成功，过早坍缩则失败。复杂度相同的模板间存在56个百分点的准确率差距。

Conclusion: 多模态感知并不保证稳健的推理能力，架构需要在冲突解决中维持持续的跨模态注意力，避免过早坍缩到单一模态。模型性能不仅取决于模态组合，更取决于模态在冲突解决中的结构性角色。

Abstract: Current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can process diverse sensory inputs, yet their reasoning remains heavily biased toward a dominant modality, resulting in brittle cross-modal reasoning. We introduce C$^3$PO, a benchmark of 3,404 samples spanning video, audio, image, and text, evaluating two abilities: information composition (fusing dispersed evidence) and counterfactual conflict (resolving deliberate contradictions). C$^3$PO's paired IC/CC structure and four-tier design enable targeted diagnosis of when and why cross-modal reasoning fails. Built through a fully automatic pipeline using 25 logically grounded templates, C$^3$PO reveals that while humans achieve 88.64% accuracy, the best model (Gemini-3.1-Pro) reaches only 73.17%, with open-source models collapsing under conflict. Through attention probes, we find 86-95% of failures stem from modality dominance: models commit to one modality while ignoring contradictory evidence, concentrating 87-95% of attention on text. Mid-layer attention entropy predicts correctness-sustained exploration succeeds, premature collapse fails. The 56-point accuracy gap between equally complex templates reveals that performance depends on modalities' structural roles in conflict resolution, not combinations. These findings show multimodal perception does not guarantee robust reasoning; architectures must enable sustained cross-modal attention to avoid premature

</details>


### [205] [Adaptive Arena-based Contestable Argumentative Network-of-Experts for Open-Ended Care Plan Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05391)
*Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Hoang-Loc Cao,Phuc Ho,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,René Richard,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CANOE是一个多智能体神经符号框架，通过五个模块（复杂度评估、自适应团队招募、基于A-QBAF的角色论证计算、人机协同质疑、护理计划合成）实现护理计划协调，支持可争议性和透明决策，并在Discharge Me!和MedicalRAG上评估了其临床正确性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 护理计划协调需要综合异构的临床、功能和心理社会信息，涉及多专业跨学科协作。传统的单一LLM流水线无法以透明且安全的方式完成这一任务，因此需要一种能够支持可解释性、可争议性和安全性的多智能体框架。

Method: 提出CANOE框架，包含五个核心模块：(1)复杂度评估模块；(2)自适应团队招募模块；(3)基于竞技场定量双极论证框架(A-QBAF)的角色论证计算模块，其中角色专门化的智能体生成支持性和攻击性论证，通过竞技场冲突消解后可信度分数在论证图中传播；(4)人机协同质疑模块，允许护理计划制定者接受、拒绝、编辑或添加论证，框架确定性重新计算最终计划；(5)护理计划合成模块。

Result: 在Discharge Me!和MedicalRAG数据集上使用ROUGE-L、AlignScore、MEDCON F1、FKGL和LLM-as-a-judge进行评估，结果显示医学微调模型在临床正确性和安全性方面表现最强，而CANOE的论证结构提供了忠实的解释和人类可争议性。
