Because the graph's candidate edges are drawn from the same embedding neighbourhood the ranker already searches, 98.6% of typed edges connect skills the ranker had already surfaced together. The graph can enrich relation semantics but cannot extend retrieval reach. We further show that evaluating on author-written queries overstates hit@5 by up to 44 points, which would have hidden these results entirely. Our contribution is a mechanistic account of why added structure does not improve retrieval over a strong ranker, and identify the conditions under which adding structural interdependence into the retrieval is optimal.

</details>


### [270] [EnvACE: Internalizing Environment Dynamics via World Rehearsal for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06197)
*Zishan Xu,Zhiyuan Yao,Yuxin Chen,Yifu Guo,Zhengxi Lu,Yuquan Lu,Jinyang Huang,Yan Xu,Yasheng Wang,Weinan Zhang,Xingshan Zeng,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnvACE是一种通过"世界排练"（world rehearsal）替代真实/合成环境交互的agentic强化学习方法，策略在训练中交替扮演行动者和环境响应者，将环境动态内化到模型参数中，在多个工具使用基准上取得优于环境扩展基线的性能，并在测试时通过私有排练进一步提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练大语言模型代理进行长时域工具使用通常依赖真实或合成的可执行环境进行交互，但这些环境的构建和验证成本高昂；外部模拟器又难以与真实世界对齐（grounding）。因此需要一种不依赖外部环境交互的替代训练范式。

Method: EnvACE采用agentic强化学习，训练中策略交替执行两个角色：(1) 行动者角色生成工具调用；(2) 环境角色对该动作生成期望的环境响应。后续决策基于排练出的响应进行。两个角色通过任务成功奖励进行端到端联合优化，从而在参数中内化动作与环境响应的映射关系，形成支持决策的agent世界模型。

Result: 在BFCL-v4、tau^2-Bench、VitaBench和FinMCP-Bench四个基准上，EnvACE实现强大且可迁移的性能，整体评估优于环境扩展（environment-scaling）基线。控制实验显示世界排练在不同模型规模下一致提升策略学习。测试时，内化的世界模型支持私有排练（private rehearsal），在适度排练预算下无需额外外部交互即可获得进一步提升。

Conclusion: 世界排练为超越外部环境约束的LLM代理训练开辟了新路径，表明将环境动态内化到策略参数中可有效替代昂贵的外部环境交互，是一种可扩展且可迁移的agent训练范式。

Abstract: Training large language model agents for long-horizon tool use typically relies on interactions with real or synthesized executable environments, whose construction and verification are costly, or on external simulators that are difficult to ground. We introduce EnvACE, an agentic reinforcement learning method that replaces external environment interaction during training with world rehearsal. The policy alternates between acting and rehearsal: it first generates a tool call, then plays the role of the environment to produce the response induced by that action, and conditions subsequent decisions on the rehearsed response. Both roles are jointly optimized end-to-end using task-success rewards. Through world rehearsal, the policy internalizes the relationship between actions and their environment responses in its parameters, yielding an agent world model that directly supports decision making. Across BFCL-v4, tau^2-Bench, VitaBench, and FinMCP-Bench, EnvACE achieves strong and transferable performance, outperforming environment-scaling baselines in the overall evaluation. Controlled studies further show that world rehearsal consistently improves policy learning across model scales. At test time, the internalized world model enables private rehearsal before committed execution, yielding further gains under a moderate rehearsal budget without additional external interaction. Our findings establish world rehearsal as a new path toward scaling LLM agent training beyond the constraints of external environments. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Within-yao/EnvACE.

</details>


### [271] [TS-RAG: Retrieval Augmented Generation for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06223)
*Yixiong Xiao,Congxi Xiao,Jingbo Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-RAG，一种将检索增强生成（RAG）思想应用于时间序列预测的框架。通过设计专门的参考令牌（reference tokens）来融合输入序列与检索到的相似序列信息，解决传统时间序列模型缺乏大语言模型生成能力、无法简单拼接检索序列的问题，在多个真实世界预测基准上取得了一致的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然RAG在增强大语言模型能力方面已证明有效，但在时间序列预测领域其应用仍然有限。时间序列模型受限于训练数据不足、参数规模小、缺乏大语言模型丰富的生成能力，直接沿用语言模型中拼接参考序列的方式可能无法获得预期效果。因此需要针对时间序列特性设计专用的RAG框架来提升预测精度。

Method: TS-RAG框架包含两个核心设计：(1) 利用RAG从外部语料库中检索与输入序列相似的时间序列作为参考；(2) 设计了专门的参考令牌（reference tokens），用于有效地融合输入序列与检索到的相似序列之间的信息，从而更稳健地捕获复杂的时间动态特征。

Result: 实验结果表明，TS-RAG在多个真实世界时间序列预测基准数据集上持续取得了最先进的性能（state-of-the-art），验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: TS-RAG证明了将RAG思路适配到时间序列预测领域是可行且有效的，通过精心设计的参考令牌而非简单拼接，成功弥补了时间序列模型在参数规模和生成能力上的局限，为时间序列预测提供了一条新的增强路径。

Abstract: While deep learning models, particularly transformer-based architectures, have shown impressive performance in time series forecasting, the application of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in this domain remains limited. Since RAG has proven effective in enhancing the capabilities of large language models by incorporating relevant external information, retrieving similar time series sequences as references might also improve accuracy in time series forecasting tasks. However, most time series models are constrained by limited training data, smaller parameter scales, and a lack of the extensive generative capabilities found in large language models. Simply concatenating reference sequences into the prompt, as done in language models, may not yield the expected results. To address these challenges, we propose a novel approach, TS-RAG, which leverages RAG to enhance forecasting performance. The framework introduces specially designed reference tokens to effectively fuse information from the input sequence with that from retrieved similar sequences, enabling a more robust capture of complex temporal dynamics. Experimental results demonstrate that TS-RAG achieves consistent state-of-the-art performance across several real-world forecasting benchmarks.

</details>


### [272] [DASH: Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons for On-Policy Self-Distillation of Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06243)
*ZhiYan Hou,Xinyu Tang,Hongyan An,Jianjin Zhang,Weizhen Wang,Yunyun Han,Gengsheng Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Haiyun Guo,Wenbin Hu,Jinqiao Wang,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons（DASH），用于解决标准on-policy自蒸馏（OPSD）中token级监督权重无法适应生成过程中教师与学生分布分歧时间演化的问题。DASH将局部蒸馏信号与序列均值之间的差距映射为自适应传播门控，通过控制反向多步聚合来动态调整token级监督权重，在三个数学推理基准和三个模型规模上均优于匹配的vanilla OPSD基线，且不增加额外的前向计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习）使用序列级稀疏奖励信号限制了推理能力的提升；OPSD通过特权教师提供密集的token级分布监督来缓解稀疏性，但标准OPSD对所有局部分歧赋予相同权重，忽略了分歧在自回归生成过程中随位置和历史演化而产生的时序上下文差异，导致无法根据实际分歧序列自适应调整监督强度。

Method: DASH将每个token的局部蒸馏信号与序列级均值之间的差距映射为自适应传播门控值，这些门控值进而控制反向的多步（multi-step）梯度聚合过程。通过这种机制，DASH能够根据生成过程中局部分歧的演化动态调整token级监督权重，从而更有效地利用教师分布信息。该方法复用OPSD已计算的教师和学生分布，无需额外的前向计算开销。

Result: 在三个数学推理基准（三个不同模型规模）上，DASH在所有实验设置下均优于匹配的vanilla OPSD重运行结果，证明了对token级监督权重进行时序自适应调整的有效性。

Conclusion: 标准OPSD因对分歧进行均匀加权而无法充分利用rollout的时序结构；DASH通过门控反向多步聚合实现了对分歧演化上下文的自适应利用，在数学推理任务上取得了稳定且一致的改进，同时保持计算成本不变。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models using automatically verifiable outcome signals, but these signals are typically sparse and at the sequence-level. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) mitigates this sparsity by querying a privileged teacher at student-visited prefixes and providing dense token-level distributional supervision. Although this dense supervision alleviates signal sparsity, we find that standard OPSD still underexploits the temporal structure of the rollout. It assigns every local divergence the same coefficient, regardless of its position or the divergence sequence in which it occurs. In on-policy autoregressive generation, the same divergence magnitude can follow different discrepancy histories, reflecting different evolutions of the mismatch between the teacher and student. Since the local scalar alone cannot distinguish these temporal contexts, standard OPSD cannot adapt its token-level weights to the realized discrepancy sequence. To address this limitation, we propose Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons (DASH). DASH maps the gap between each local distillation signal and the sequence-level mean to an adaptive propagation gate and then uses these gates to control backward multi-step aggregation. By doing so, DASH adjusts token-level supervision weights according to how local divergences evolve during generation. Experiments on three mathematical reasoning benchmarks across three model scales show that DASH improves over our matched vanilla OPSD reruns on every benchmark at all three scales. DASH reuses the teacher and student distributions that OPSD already computes, so the gains require no additional teacher or student forward pass.
  Code: https://github.com/DBtxy/DASH-OPSD

</details>


### [273] [Improving the Realism of Synthetic Clinical Benchmarks Under Utility Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06265)
*Omid Bazgir,Md Nasir,Jacob Hoffman,Yang Yang,Manu Agrawal,Anusua Trivedi,Vinay Rao Dandin,Chris Gibbons,Christine Swisher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在保持企业AI代理下游效用检查通过的前提下，改进合成临床基准的真实性。作者将基准修订形式化为"效用约束下的真实性提升"问题，在基于Synthea生成的care-gap基准上验证，通过两种确定性修订方案在维持效用底线的同时显著提升真实性的多个维度，并指出内部真实性与对运营数据的源保真度是相关但不同的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成临床基准虽然能通过效用检查，但在结构上仍不真实，尤其在隐私敏感的医疗场景中运营数据难以获取。作者希望在不打破现有下游效用检查的前提下提升基准的真实性，因为仅仅通过效用检查不足以证明基准的真实性。

Method: 将基准修订形式化为效用约束下的真实性优化问题（数据变更需提升真实性且保持在运营效用底线之上）。在Synthea生成患者数据并通过电子健康记录演示工作流处理的care-gap基准上，从缺失结构、简洁性、结构合理性、人群对齐四个维度衡量真实性，提出两种确定性修订方案，并与朴素稠密化对照组进行比较。

Result: 基线基准极度"单薄"：采样对缺失率79.44%，仅12.75%行可操作，38.94%患者零可操作性度量，前三token集中度达100.0%。两种确定性修订在保持效用底线的同时改善了这些真实性指标，而朴素稠密化对照组保留了不真实的模板化模式。此外发现内部基准真实性与对聚合运营参考数据的源保真度是相关但不同的目标。

Conclusion: 合成基准质量应被显式优化，效用仅应被视为一个约束条件而非真实性的充分证据。在隐私敏感的医疗场景中，需要通过显式真实性目标来指导基准修订。

Abstract: Synthetic clinical benchmarks for enterprise AI agents can pass existing utility checks and still remain structurally unrealistic, especially in privacy-sensitive healthcare settings where operational data are hard to access. We study how to improve such benchmarks without breaking the downstream utility checks already used in practice.
  We formulate benchmark revision as utility-constrained realism improvement: dataset changes should increase realism while staying above an operational utility floor. We instantiate this idea on a care-gap benchmark derived from Synthea-generated patients exercised through demonstration electronic health record workflows and then processed by the same downstream pipeline as operational data. Realism is measured through missingness structure, simplicity, structural plausibility, and population alignment.
  The baseline benchmark is extremely thin: sampled-pair missingness is 79.44%, only 12.75% of rows are actionable, 38.94% of patients have zero actionable measures, and top-three token concentration reaches 100.0%. Two deterministic revisions improve these panels while remaining above the current utility floor, whereas a naive densification control preserves unrealistic templating. We further show that internal benchmark realism and source fidelity to an aggregate operational reference are related but distinct objectives. These results suggest that synthetic benchmark quality should be optimized explicitly, with utility treated as one constraint rather than as sufficient evidence of realism.

</details>


### [274] [The Illusion of Visual Tool-Use: A Causal Audit of Thinking with Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06270)
*Zhiheng Wang,Bo Peng,Lai Wei,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果审计框架（CauAudit），从三个层级（策略、轨迹、步骤）对多模态大模型"以图思考"视觉工具调用进行因果干预分析，发现尽管聚合准确率有提升，但视觉证据在大多数滚动轨迹中不具因果有效性，存在"调用但不看"和"看了但无规划"两类失败模式，揭示了"视觉工具使用的幻觉"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉工具调用（如裁剪放大）相比直接推理往往仅有边际收益甚至负收益，且会重复裁剪无关区域、在直接推理本可答对的问题上失败。作者质疑工具返回的视觉证据是否真的因果性地影响了答案，而非仅仅作为虚假相关信号。

Method: 将视觉工具使用形式化为因果图，区分观察介导路径与行动诱导捷径；在三个层级做干预审计：策略层（对比工具调用与直接推理）、轨迹层（扰动整个rollout中的全部观察）、步骤层（在固定前缀下反事实替换单个观察）。步骤层估计量"Visual Evidence Gain"用于隔离每个返回观察的因果贡献。

Result: 在6个代表性模型和5个细粒度感知基准上，发现策略校准失调的两类失败模式：Calling Without Looking（观察无因果效应）和Looking Without Planning（观察有信息但调用日程不连贯）。轨迹级诊断将策略级准确率增益分解后显示，增益集中在少数校准样本上，即在广泛rollout范围内视觉工具使用并非因果有效。

Conclusion: 尽管存在聚合准确率提升，视觉工具使用在大多数情况下不具因果效力，作者将其称为"视觉工具使用的幻觉"。该工作揭示了视觉工具调用在因果层面上的虚假有效性，为审计和改进此类系统提供了方法论框架。

Abstract: The "thinking-with-images" paradigm equips multimodal LLMs with active visual operations such as crop-and-zoom. However, models using these operations often achieve only marginal or negative gains over direct inference at substantially higher token cost. They may also repeatedly crop irrelevant regions and fail on questions that direct inference answers correctly. We ask whether the returned visual evidence causally affects the answer. To answer this question, we formulate visual tool-use as a causal graph that separates observation-mediated paths from action-induced shortcuts. We then audit it through interventions at the three levels: policy (comparing tool-use with direct inference), trajectory (corrupting all observations during rollout), and step (counterfactually replacing one individual observation under a fixed prefix). Our step-level estimand, Visual Evidence Gain, isolates the contribution of each returned observation. Across six representative models and five fine-grained perception benchmarks, we uncover policy miscalibration with two failure modes. In Calling Without Looking, returned observations have no causal effect on the answer. In Looking Without Planning, observations are informative but the call schedule is incoherent. A trajectory-level diagnostic decomposes the policy-level accuracy gain and shows that the gain is concentrated in a Calibrated minority. We term this discrepancy the illusion of visual tool-use: despite aggregate accuracy gains, visual tool-use is not causally effective across a broad range of rollouts. The code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/CauAudit.

</details>


### [275] [QuanTiMedAI: Quantum-Enhanced Time-Series Model guided by Agentic AI for Cardiac Arrest Mortality Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06294)
*Mutasim Fuad Sarker,Adiba Rahman Namira,Wafa Binte Alam,Md Adnan Arefeen,Mahzabeen Emu,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QuanTiMedAI框架，结合智能体大语言模型（LLM）进行临床特征发现与紧凑量子循环网络进行时序感知的心搏骤停死亡率预测，在MIMIC-IV队列上以605个参数实现AUROC 0.852，较当前最优基线提升约2.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心搏骤停在ICU中致死率极高，但现有的死亡率预测研究多依赖入院早期的静态摘要，忽略了患者整个ICU住院期间生理恶化与恢复的时间动态变化，从而限制了对患者预后的准确评估。

Method: QuanTiMedAI框架由两部分组成：一是智能体大语言模型（LLM），用于进行临床信息驱动的特征发现与选择；二是紧凑型量子循环网络，用于时序感知的死亡率预测，通过非线性特征增强实现低参数量的高性能建模。实验基于MIMIC-IV心搏骤停患者队列，并进行了结构化消融研究以验证各设计选择。

Result: 智能体LLM引导的特征选择优于传统特征选择方法；量子架构在仅605个参数的情况下实现AUROC 0.852，较当前SOTA基线提升约2.9%；消融实验系统验证了各架构组件的贡献。量子增强序列建模在参数远少于经典循环网络的条件下超越了经典循环网络。

Conclusion: 量子增强的序列建模能够在保持极低参数量的同时超越经典循环网络性能，为重症监护中的时序死亡率预测提供了一种可行的新范式。

Abstract: Cardiac arrest remains one of the most lethal conditions encountered in intensive care units. Despite the growing availability of electronic health record data, existing mortality prediction studies in this population largely depend on static summaries derived from early admission. Such approaches ignore the temporal progression of physiological deterioration and recovery that unfolds throughout a patient's ICU stay. To address this limitation, we introduce QuanTiMedAI, a quantum-agentic framework developed for cardiac arrest mortality prediction using agentic AI guided quantum enhancement time series model. The proposed system combines an agentic large language model (LLM) for clinically informed feature discovery with a compact quantum recurrent network for temporality aware mortality prediction. Our findings demonstrate that agentic LLM-guided feature selection consistently outperforms conventional feature selection approaches, and the proposed quantum architecture achieves competitive predictive performance through nonlinear feature enhancement while keeping the number of parameters very low. Through extensive experimentation on a MIMIC-IV cohort of cardiac arrest patients, QuanTiMedAI's quantum-enhanced architecture attains an AUROC of 0.852 using only 605 parameters, an improvement of approximately 2.9\% over a current state-of-the-art baseline for this task. A structured ablation study systematically validates the contribution of each architectural design choice. These results show that quantum-enhanced sequential modeling can exceed classical recurrent networks while using substantially fewer parameters.

</details>


### [276] [Bias Analysis of L2 Speaking Assessment Systems Using Concept Activation Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06300)
*Arya Labroo,Mengjie Qian,Kate Knill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了基于概念激活向量（CAV）的偏见分析到两个基于Transformer的L2口语自动评估系统（BERT文本评分器和基于Whisper的多模态评分器），并探索使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）在稀疏潜空间中学习更清晰的概念方向。研究发现概念可恢复性强烈依赖于被探测的表征和架构，而非概念本身；SAE虽使概念更线性可恢复，但在低维层中会减弱原始激活空间的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动口语评估系统在高风险场景中的部署要求其评分依赖于口语能力而非不相关的说话者属性（如母语L1或年龄）。虽然基于Transformer的基础模型提高了口语评分准确性，但其黑箱表征使公平性和可解释性分析更加困难，迫切需要有效的方法来审计这些系统中的偏见。

Method: 借鉴先前使用CAV检测基于特征评分器中不期望属性偏见的工���，将CAV分析扩展到两种神经口语评估系统：基于BERT的文本评分器和基于Whisper的语音-文本多模态评分器。CAV将人类可解释的概念表示为模型激活空间中的方向，并通过基于梯度的敏感性指标区分概念是否被编码于内部表征与是否影响预测分数。此外，由于CAV依赖线性可分性而在复杂神经嵌入空间中较难实现，还研究了通过SAE在稀疏潜空间学习CAV并将其映射回激活空间以获得更清晰概念方向的方法。

Result: 分析表明概念的可恢复性强烈依赖于被探测的表征和架构，而非仅取决于概念本身；对概念的敏感性也具有架构依赖性。SAE使概念更易于线性恢复，但会减弱原始激活空间中的敏感性，尤其是在低维层中表现尤为显著。

Conclusion: 这些发现强调了在审计口语评估系统偏见时，需要区分概念可恢复性与概念影响力，而不能将两者混为一谈；这一区分对于可靠地检测和缓解自动口语评估系统中的偏见至关重要。

Abstract: Automatic speaking assessment systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings to mark second language (L2) learners' speaking tests, making it critical to show that their scores depend on speaking proficiency rather than irrelevant speaker attributes such as first language (L1) or age. Transformer-based foundation models have improved the accuracy of these L2 speaking graders, but their black-box representations make fairness and interpretability analysis more difficult. Building on prior work that used Concept Activation Vectors (CAVs) to detect bias towards unwanted attributes (`concepts') in feature-based graders, we extend CAV-based analysis to two neural speaking assessment systems: a text-based BERT grader and a speech-and-text multimodal grader based on Whisper. CAVs represent human-interpretable concepts as directions in a model's activation space, allowing us to distinguish between whether a concept is encoded in a model's internal representations and whether it influences the predicted score, the latter quantified using a gradient-based sensitivity metric. Since CAVs rely on linear separability, which is less likely in complex neural embedding spaces, we also investigate whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide cleaner concept directions by learning CAVs in a sparse latent space and mapping them back to activation space. Our analysis shows that concept recoverability depends strongly on the representation and architecture being probed, rather than on the concept alone. Sensitivity to concepts is also architecture-dependent. SAEs make concepts more linearly recoverable, but attenuate the original activation-space sensitivity, especially in low-dimensional layers. These findings highlight the need to distinguish concept recoverability from concept influence when auditing bias in speaking assessment systems.

</details>


### [277] [TRAJDEBUG: Tracing Error Lifecycle to Identify Critical Failures in Long-Horizon Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06346)
*Yunjia Qi,Zehua Yin,Xintong Shi,Hao Peng,Songyuanyi Lu,Yixian Liu,Richeng Xuan,Yuhong Liu,Zhichao Hu,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Bin Xu,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajDebug框架，用于在LLM智能体的长失败轨迹中定位最早的关键错误步骤。它通过多粒度历史压缩和基于证据的错误识别解决长轨迹错误发现难题，并通过追踪错误生命周期进行关键归因。作者还构建了包含486条人工标注失败轨迹的TrajErrBench基准，实验表明TrajDebug优于现有基线，其诊断结果可有效改善下游智能体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体系统在复杂领域表现优异，但存在级联错误和调试困难的问题。关键错误检测旨在定位失败轨迹中最终导致失败的早期错误步骤，但面临两大挑战：一是长轨迹中的证据分散在不同指令、观察和上下文之间，难以识别个体错误；二是失败轨迹中常包含多个具有不同下游影响的局部错误，只有部分对最终失败负有责任。这两大挑战亟需一种能够系统化定位并归因关键错误的方法。

Method: TrajDebug框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 错误发现模块：采用多粒度历史压缩（multi-granularity history compression）来处理长轨迹，并结合基于证据的错误识别（evidence-based error identification）来定位个体错误；(2) 错误归因模块：通过追踪每个错误的解析状态（resolution status）和终端影响（terminal impact）来识别对最终失败负有关键责任的错误，构建完整的错误生命周期（error-lifecycle）追踪。此外，作者构建了TrajErrBench基准，包含486条来自Tau2Bench和SWE-Bench Pro的人工标注失败轨迹，覆盖工具使用和编码场景。

Result: 在多个智能体基准上的实验表明，TrajDebug在整体性能上优于现有基线。此外，应用研究表明其诊断结果能够为下游智能体提供可操作的反馈，从而提升下游智能体的成功率。

Conclusion: TrajDebug通过错误生命周期追踪范式有效解决了长轨迹中的关键错误检测与归因问题，为LLM智能体调试提供了可靠的诊断工具。作者将公开代码和数据以促进后续研究。

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems have shown remarkable capabilities in complex domains, while suffering from cascading errors and difficulty in debugging. Critical error detection aims to locate the earliest error step in a failed trajectory that is responsible for the final failure. However, progress faces two main challenges. First, long trajectories make it difficult to identify individual errors, since the evidence for judging a step may be scattered across distant instructions, observations, and prior context. Second, failed trajectories often contain multiple local errors with different downstream effects, only some of which remain responsible for the final failure. In this work, we propose TrajDebug, an error-lifecycle tracing framework that addresses long-trajectory error discovery with multi-granularity history compression and evidence-based error identification, and supports critical attribution by tracing each error's resolution status and terminal impact. We further construct TrajErrBench, a benchmark of 486 manually annotated failed trajectories from Tau2Bench and SWE-Bench Pro, covering realistic tool-use and coding scenarios. Experiments across diverse agent benchmarks show that TrajDebug achieves the best overall performance over existing baselines, and application studies further demonstrate that its diagnoses provide actionable feedback for improving downstream agent success. We will release the codes and data to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [278] [Challenges in Evaluating Explanation Methods for Static and Evolving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06351)
*Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对可解释人工智能（XAI）评估不足的局限性展开研究，以DetoxAI图像识别系统为案例，提出基于人类评估的解释方法评价方案，并探讨了概念漂移下自适应反事实解释的方法及其与数据、模型、解释共同演化挑战的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法在评估方面存在明显不足，缺乏系统化的人类公信力评估，也未能充分应对现实场景中数据流不断变化（概念漂移）的挑战，导致解释方法在实际部署中的可信度和适应性受限。

Method: 以DetoxAI图像识别系统（用于偏差检测和概念遗忘）为例，展示XAI评估不足的问题；提出人类公信力评估（human-grounded evaluation）方法用于评价图像分类解释；探索针对概念漂移数据流的解释自适应方法，并讨论反事实解释在此问题中的适配经验。

Result: 通过案例研究展示了XAI评估不足的具体表现，提出了人类公信力评估的实践示例，并验证了反事实解释在概念漂移场景中的适配潜力，同时揭示了解释需要与数据和模型协同演化的挑战。

Conclusion: XAI方法的发展需要在评估框架、动态环境适应性以及数据-模型-解释三者协同演化等方面持续改进，以提升其在实际应用中的可靠性和价值。

Abstract: This paper addresses the limitations of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) with respect to insufficient evaluation. They are illustrated through the DetoxAI image recognition system for bias detection and concept unlearning. Then, an example of a human-grounded evaluation of methods for explaining image classification is presented. The paper further explores methods for adapting explanations to evolving data streams with concept drift. Experiences with adapting counterfactuals for this problem are discussed. Finally it is related to the challenges of tracking the co-evolution of data, models, and explanations.\footnote{This paper has been accepted for a publication in J.Nalepa (ed) Explainable AI in Space. Proceedings of EASi 2026 Workshop at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 Bremen, Springer CCIS vol 3107 (2016).}

</details>


### [279] [The Low Frequency Trap: Video Language Models Fail at Simple Event Bookkeeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06361)
*Sarvesh Baskar,Zikui Cai,Shayan Shabihi,Anirudh Satheesh,Muhammad R. Islam,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于可执行轨迹（trace-grounded）的参数化剖析方法，用于视频事件计数任务的诊断评估。通过控制事件数量N和频率F生成2,190段视频，在三个任务（弹球撞击、眨眼、状态转移）中揭示了一个分阶段的时序失败模式：事件表征决定了模型能否初步获取证据，且该限制随计数和频率增加而加剧。在高计数高频区间，仅0.2%的最终计数正确，模型仅恢复18.1%的真实事件。提高采样率虽能提升表面准确率（19.6%→29.3%），但事件序列与真值一致率仅3.7%，说明额外帧会虚增分数而不产生忠实的事件恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实世界视频基准虽然覆盖面广，但其固定片段将事件数量、频率、时长和视觉复杂度纠缠在一起，导致故障模式难以隔离。已有的程序化基准虽然提供更好的控制，但只对最终答案评分，无法将报告的事件与可执行的真实事件轨迹进行对照审计，缺乏细粒度的诊断能力。

Method: 提出轨迹接地参数化剖析（trace-grounded parametric profiling）方法：在保持渲染固定的条件下，系统地改变事件数量N和频率F，生成2,190段视频覆盖三个受控任务（弹球撞击、视觉眨眼、分类状态转移）。每段视频附带可执行事件轨迹，支持能力曲面估计（capability-surface estimation）和时间戳级评估。此外通过提高采样率、改变提示策略等手段测试瓶颈来源。

Result: 在80%可靠性阈值下，Gemini 3.6 Flash对持久状态转移在0.5和1.0 Hz下可靠计数至12个事件，但对瞬态眨眼事件无可靠的正向计数区域。高计数高频区间正确率仅0.2%，事件恢复率仅18.1%。提高采样率将弹球准确率从19.6%提升至29.3%，但序列一致率仅3.7%。不同提示策略增益有限，真实世界视频评估同样呈现成功率集中于低事件计数的现象。

Conclusion: 事件表征方式决定了模型最初是否能访问证据，该限制随计数和频率增加而持续恶化。事件计数评估应从聚合准确率指标转向详细的诊断性剖析，以定位时序推理的失败环节。

Abstract: Real-world video benchmarks provide broad coverage, but their fixed clips entangle event count, rate, duration, and visual complexity, making failure modes hard to isolate. While existing programmatic benchmarks offer better control, they score only the final answer rather than auditing reported events against executable ground truth. To bridge this gap, we introduce trace-grounded parametric profiling for event counting in three controlled video tasks: bouncing-ball wall contacts, visual blinks, and categorical state transitions. Across 2,190 videos, we vary event count N and frequency F while holding rendering fixed. Each video includes an executable event trace for capability-surface estimation and timestamp-level evaluation. Our results reveal a staged temporal failure. At an 80% reliability threshold, Gemini 3.6 Flash reliably counts persistent state transitions up to 12 events at 0.5 and 1.0 Hz, yet demonstrates no reliable positive-count region for transient blinking events. Thus, event representation dictates whether a model initially accesses evidence -- a limitation that compounds as count and frequency increase. In the high-count, high-frequency regime, only 0.2% of final counts are correct and the model recovers just 18.1% of true events. To test if visual access is the primary bottleneck, we increase sampling rate. Although this boosts Bounce Ball accuracy from 19.6% to 29.3%, the reported sequence agrees with ground truth only 3.7% of the time. Extra frames can therefore inflate final scores without producing faithful event recovery. Different prompting strategies yield similarly limited gains, and real-world video evaluations show the same concentration of success at low event counts. Ultimately, trace-grounded profiling shifts video evaluation from aggregate accuracy metrics to a detailed diagnostic of where temporal reasoning fails.

</details>


### [280] [Tracing the Heart: An Evidence-Linked Pipeline for Heart-Failure Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06366)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Michelle Hu,Dorisa Shehi,Daniel Kang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Koustuv Saha,Christian Poellabauer,Christopher Lee,Sajeev Singh,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Zeeshan Ahmed,Priyadarshini Kachroo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Nimblemind多智能体系统（nMAS），一个基于证据链接和规则编排的自动化电子健康记录（EHR）特征工程流水线，专门用于心力衰竭表型分析。在500条模拟患者记录上，nMAS生成了132个结构化特征和70个规则评分的聚合特征，将HFrEF表型AUROC从0.895提升至0.963，HFpEF从0.870提升至0.910，且特征通过独立LLM评估达到81.5%的评分，展示了自动化特征工程的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR特征工程是临床研究和AI领域的重大瓶颈，占数据科学家39-45%的工作量。在心力衰竭领域尤为突出（影响670万美国成年人），需要整合分散的EHR数据并融入疾病特定的循证临床推理。现有的基于规则和大语言模型的方法只能提供部分自动化，且可维护性和证据可追溯性有限，亟需一种更自动、可审计且可维护的特征工程方案。

Method: 开发了Nimblemind多智能体系统（nMAS），这是一个基于证据链接和规则编排的自动化特征工程流水线。该系统从9个EHR源表中提取数据，通过多智能体协作生成结构化和规则评分的聚合特征，并对特征进行结构完整性、规则合规性和来源验证，同时使用受限LLM进行审计。实验使用500条模拟心力衰竭患者记录进行验证。

Result: nMAS生成了132个结构化特征和70个规则评分的聚合特征。添加聚合特征后，HFrEF表型的held-out AUROC从0.895提升到0.963，HFpEF从0.870提升到0.910。独立LLM基于规则的评估显示特征在证据支持和方法学合理性方面获得了最大评分的81.5%。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在复杂心血管EHR数据上进行自动化、可审计的特征工程是可行的。但研究仅限于单一机构队列评估，需要外部验证来进一步确认方法的泛化性。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) feature engineering is a major bottleneck in clinical research and AI, accounting for 39-45% of data scientists' workload. This is especially pronounced in heart failure, which affects an estimated 6.7 million U.S. adults and requires integrating fragmented EHR data with disease-specific, guideline-based clinical reasoning. Existing rule-based and large language model (LLM)-based approaches offer only partial automation with limited maintainability and evidence traceability. We developed the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), an evidence-linked, rubric-grounded pipeline for automated heart-failure feature engineering, and evaluated it on 500 dummy patient records from nine EHR source tables. nMAS generated 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features, verified for structural integrity, rubric compliance, and provenance, and audited by a restricted LLM. Adding the aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF phenotyping, and an independent LLM-based rubric assessment of evidence support and methodological soundness scored the features at 81.5% of maximum points. These results demonstrate the feasibility of automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data, though evaluation was limited to a single-institution cohort and external validation is needed.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [281] [Computationally Efficient Collaborative Communication Via Regularity-Based Coarsening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05327)
*Mark Bedaywi,Scott Emmons,Nika Haghtalab,Stuart Russell*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文证明在通信博弈中，只要存在一个短的高效用协议，就足以实现高效通信。给出了一个多项式时间算法，在任意目标效用α下，设计一个达到至少α-ε效用的协议，仅需2^{O(CC_α(G))}/ε²比特通信，并证明该指数依赖是最优的（除非P=NP）。技术上依赖对Frieze-Kannan弱正则引理的强化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体信息聚合领域的先前工作依赖于信息替代性或弱可学习性等结构假设，这些假设过于严格。作者希望填补这一空白：仅凭存在短高效用协议（CC_α(G)有界）这一较弱条件，是否就足以保证高效的协议设计？

Method: （1）提出一个poly(n,m,1/ε)时间的算法，构造达到α-ε效用的协议，通信复杂度为2^{O(CC_α(G))}/ε²比特。（2）通过归约证明除非P=NP，任何多项式时间算法都无法使用少于2^{CC_α(G)-2}比特找到最优协议，表明指数依赖紧致。（3）技术上，强化了Frieze-Kannan弱正则引理，构造了一个博弈G的coarsening（将观测空间划分为常数大小分区的Ĝ），使得G与Ĝ对所有短通信协议不可区分。

Result: 给出了高效的协议设计算法，其通信复杂度随CC_α(G)指数增长，并证明了该指数依赖是最优的。同时表明，先前工作中使用的信息替代性和弱可学习性等结构假设实际上已经蕴含CC_α(G)=O(1)，因此是比本文所需条件更严格的限制。

Conclusion: 存在短的高效用协议这一条件本身就是以支持高效的多智能体信息聚合通信，无需更严格的结构假设。这一结果显著弱化了先前工作的前提，并通过新的coarsening定理提供了有力的多项式时间变换工具，该定理本身可能具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: Our results show that the existence of a short high-utility protocol already suffices for efficient communication. In particular, in a game with $n$ possible observations and $m$ actions: (1) For any achievable target utility $α$, we give an algorithm with $\mathrm{poly}(n, m, 1/ε)$ runtime that designs a protocol achieving utility at least $α-ε$ using only $2^{\mathcal O(CC_α(G))}/ε^2$ bits of communication. Here, $CC_α(G)$ is the minimum number of bits used by any protocol, even a computationally inefficient one, to achieve utility $α$. (2) We prove that this exponential dependence on $CC_α(G)$ is tight up to a constant. That is, unless $\mathrm P=\mathrm{NP}$, no polynomial-time algorithm can in general find optimal protocols using fewer than $2^{CC_α(G) -2}$ bits.
  We note that our results strictly weaken the assumptions required by prior work in the multi-agent information aggregation literature, filling a gap that had remained elusive even for games with constant $CC_α(G)$. In particular, prior guarantees for agreement-based information aggregation rely on structural assumptions such as informational substitutes or weak learnability. We show that these assumptions already imply $CC_α(G) = O(1)$ and are therefore more restrictive conditions than required by our protocol to succeed.
  On a technical level, our results involve a novel strengthening of the Frieze-Kannan weak regularity lemma and yield the following powerful polynomial-time transformation tool: for every communication game $G$, it constructs a game $\hat G$ that is a coarsening of the agents' observation spaces into constant-size partitions, such that $G$ and $\hat G$ are indistinguishable with respect to every short communication protocol. This coarsening theorem is the engine behind our algorithm and may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [282] [A Polynomial-Time Rule Satisfying Full Justified Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05397)
*Fabian Frank,Jannik Peters*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种满足完全正当代表（FJR）的Greedy Justified Candidate规则变体，该变体可在多项式时间内计算，解决了FJR委员会能否高效计算的开放问题；同时否定了MES返回的委员会总能扩展以满足FJR的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于批准的多人投票中，按比例代表少数群体是核心目标之一。FJR是一种期望的代表性概念，但此前其可计算性未知，且MES委员会的可扩展性也未有定论，这两个开放问题亟需解答。

Method: 提出Greedy Justified Candidate规则的一种变体，该变体被证明满足FJR且可在多项式时间内计算；同时通过构造反例证明MES返回的委员会不一定能扩展至满足FJR。

Result: 1) 新的GJC变体在多项式时间内可计算且满足FJR，回答了FJR委员会能否高效计算的问题；2) 以否定方式回答了MES委员会能否扩展至FJR的开放问题。

Conclusion: FJR委员会可以在多项式时间内被有效计算，而MES生成的委员会不具备可扩展至FJR的通用性质，这两项结论为按比例多人投票规则的设计提供了重要的理论边界。

Abstract: In approval-based multiwinner voting, voters submit an approval ballot on which basis a committee of fixed size $k$ has to be selected. Compared to single-winner voting this makes it possible to represent minorities in the selection of the committee as well. Thus, one of the most desirable goals of multiwinner voting is to satisfy proportional representation. In this paper we propose a variant of the Greedy Justified Candidate rule that satisfy the notion of Full Justified Representation (FJR). This variant can be computed in polynomial time answering the open question of whether an FJR committee can always be computed efficiently. Additionally, we answer an open question whether committees returned by MES can always be extended in a way to satisfy FJR negatively.

</details>


### [283] [Full Justified Representation under Hare and Droop Quotas in Polynomial Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05417)
*Yizhou Ai*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究批准式多赢家选举中的完全公正代表（FJR）公理，在Hare和Droop两种配额约定下分别提出了一个递减预算算法，能确定性构造满足相应FJR公理的委员会，且复杂度为O(kmn)次有理运算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全公正代表（FJR）是批准式多赢家选举中比EJR更强的代表性公理，如何在Hare与Droop两种配额下有效构造满足FJR的委员会此前缺乏系统性的算法方法。

Method: 提出一个递减预算（descending-budget）算法：每位选民在其当前代表性差距（representation gap）上分配剩余预算，每当累积的出价达到共同价格时即购买对应候选人。通过设置两种不同的候选人价格λ_H=n/k（Hare）和λ_D=n/(k+1)（Droop），分别得到Hare-FJR和Droop-FJR委员会。两种情形共享历史支付不变式和终末行列核算论证，Droop情形额外需要处理全部k个席位填满时的剩余预算论证。算法在选民和候选人顺序固定时是确定性的，复杂度为O(kmn)次有理运算。

Result: 算法在Hare配额下返回Hare-FJR委员会，在Droop配额下返回满足更强Droop-FJR公理（由Casey和Elkind提出）的委员会；两种变体均为确定性算法，复杂度为O(kmn)有理运算。

Conclusion: 本文通过统一的递减预算算法框架同时解决了Hare与Droop配额下的FJR构造问题，其中Droop情形需要额外的剩余预算论证来处理全部席位被填满的边界情况，证明了这两种FJR概念均可通过高效确定性算法实现。

Abstract: I study Full Justified Representation (FJR) in approval-based multiwinner elections under both the Hare and Droop quota conventions. I introduce a descending-budget algorithm in which voters distribute their remaining budgets across their current representation gaps and candidates are purchased whenever the resulting offers cover a common price. With candidate price $λ_H=n/k$, the algorithm returns a Hare-FJR committee; with candidate price $λ_D=n/(k+1)$, it returns a committee satisfying the more demanding Droop-FJR axiom of Casey and Elkind. The two guarantees share a historical-payment invariant and a terminal row--column accounting argument, while the Droop proof requires a new residual-budget argument when all $k$ paid seats are filled. Both variants are deterministic once the voter and candidate orders are fixed and use $O(kmn)$ rational operations.

</details>


### [284] [AI-Farol: Co-Evolutionary Dynamics in a Multi-Agent Two-Sided Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05479)
*Iosif Polenakis,Kalliopi Kastampolidou,Theodore Andronikos*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文将经典的El Farol酒吧博弈重构为双面学习系统：酒吧不再是被动的容量约束，而是具备AI学习能力的战略主体，通过动态定价主动调节协调过程，并与部分可观测环境下使用AI学习的代理共同演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统El Farol酒吧博弈将场地视为被动的容量约束，忽略了现实机构（如平台、服务商）通常具有主动调节能力。作者希望通过将酒吧建模为战略主体，弥补原有框架中机构角色的缺失，更真实地反映复杂适应系统中协调机制与制度设计之间的交互。

Method: 论文在两方面扩展原始框架：(1) 引入部分可观测性，代理仅能观察过往参与者的子集；(2) 将酒吧从被动容量阈值转变为主动机制设计者，通过策略学习优化动态定价，在收益、利用率与可持续性约束之间寻求平衡。代理使用AI学习形成信念并适应不完整信息下的参与策略，酒吧则采用策略学习优化定价，构成双面学习系统。

Result: 将协调过程建模为有限理性代理与自适应制度之间的共同演化过程，为拥堵管理、资源分配和复杂适应系统中的机制设计提供了新的分析视角与见解。

Conclusion: 该框架表明，机构性调节者（酒吧）的主动性学习与代理的适应性学习共同塑造协调结果，为理解现实世界中平台经济、共享资源管理等场景下的双边学习与制度演化提供了理论模型。

Abstract: The El Farol Bar game is a classical model of coordination under uncertainty that traditionally treats the venue as a passive constraint. In this work, we reconceptualize the problem by modeling the bar as a strategic player endowed with AI-driven learning capabilities. We extend the original framework in two principal directions: first, by introducing partial observability, whereby agents observe only subsets of past attendees; and second, by transforming the bar from a passive capacity threshold into an active mechanism designer that adjusts pricing policies to balance revenue, utilization, and sustainability constraints. Agents employ AI-based learning to form beliefs and adapt attendance strategies under incomplete information, while the bar applies policy learning to optimize dynamic pricing. The resulting two-sided learning system frames coordination as a co-evolutionary process between boundedly rational agents and an adaptive institution, offering insights into congestion management, resource allocation, and mechanism design in complex adaptive systems.

</details>


### [285] [Candidate Resignation Monotonicity in Approval-Based Committee Elections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06156)
*Yeeseok Oh,Dominik Peters*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于审批的委员会选举中当选成员辞职时的"辞职单调性"公理，发现许多主流规则不满足该性质，且JR公理与辞职单调性不相容；提出了基于最大流的积分规则（最大支付规则MPR）以满足表示公理和放松的辞职单调性，并进一步分析了候选人通过引入弱候选人进行策略性操控的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际中当选成员可能辞职，需要重新运行选举规则，但现有ABC选举规则未考虑这一场景下结果的一致性和公平性。作者希望形式化"辞职单调性"这一性质，识别现有规则的不足，并设计能同时满足表示公理与辞职单调性的新规则。

Method: 作者形式化定义了辞职单调性公理；通过将问题与动态候选集上的ABC选举工作联系起来，证明了JR公理与辞职单调性不相容；基于选举实例的网络流表示，设计了积分的最大支付规则（MPR），满足PJR+公理和放松形式的辞职单调性；最后分析了一个新的策略性博弈场景，考察不同规则对该操控的免疫力。

Result: 众多重要ABC规则（如PAV等）不满足辞职单调性；JR与辞职单调性不相容；基于最大流的MPR规则可同时满足表示公理与放松的辞职单调性，但一般情形下NP-hard，仅在结构化领域可计算；所有辞职单调规则及多种序列规则对"引入弱候选人"的操控免疫，而PAV规则可被此类操控。

Conclusion: 辞职单调性是一个有价值但难以实现的性质，它强化了对ABC规则设计的约束；最大支付规则为兼顾表示公理和辞职稳定性提供了新方向，但也揭示了公平性与计算复杂性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Approval-based committee (ABC) elections select a fixed-size committee of candidates based on voters' approval preferences. We study a setting where elected members may resign and formalize resignation monotonicity: when we re-run the voting rule after resignations, a resignation monotone rule must still elect all remaining previously winning candidates. We show that many prominent ABC rules fail this property, and by connecting our notion to prior work on ABC elections with dynamic candidate sets, we show that the justified representation (JR) axiom is incompatible with resignation monotonicity. It turns out that fractional committee voting does not suffer from this issue: rules based on using maximum flows in the network representation of an election instance can satisfy both representation axioms and resignation monotonicity. We design an integral version of maximum flow, the Maximum Payment Rule (MPR), and show that it satisfies a relaxed form of resignation monotonicity that only requires there to be some way of replacing the resigning candidates in a way that guarantees the PJR+ axiom. MPR is NP-hard to compute in general, but becomes tractable in structured domains. Finally, we study a strategic setting where a losing candidate may introduce new weak candidates to the election to try to become a winner. We show that all resignation monotone rules as well as many sequential rules are immune, but that the PAV rule can be manipulated in this way.

</details>


### [286] [Fair and Efficient Balanced Allocations for Additive Valuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06325)
*Benjamin Cookson,Nisarg Shah,Paritosh Verma*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了不可分割商品在平衡约束（任意两个agent的束大小相差最多1）下的公平与效率分配问题，证明了对于任意可加估值，存在同时满足EF1和fPO的平衡分配，并将结果推广到类别（分区拟阵）约束场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的Kawase等人（2026）仅在个性化双值估值或最多两种估值类型的场景下证明了平衡分配的EF1+fPO存在性。本文旨在放宽这一限制，为任意加性估值建立更一般的结果。

Method: 将Knaster-Kuratowski-Mazurkiewicz（KKM）引理应用于加权福利对偶框架，并发展了一个新颖的价格穿插引理，以克服先前工作中的障碍。该技术进一步扩展到分区拟阵约束场景。

Result: 对于任意加性估值，证明了平衡分配中EF1与fPO可以同时满足。在类别（分区拟阵）约束下，证明了存在满足类别敏感的EF1松弛版本（每种类别移除至多一个商品即可消除嫉妒）的fPO分配。

Conclusion: 通过KKM引理和价格穿插引理这一新的技术工具，成功解决了任意加性估值下平衡分配的公平效率兼容性问题，并展示了该方法对更一般约束的适用性。

Abstract: We study the existence of fair and efficient allocations of indivisible goods under the balancedness constraint, which requires that any two agents' bundles differ in size by at most one. Our main result establishes the existence of balanced allocations that satisfy envy-freeness up to one good (EF1) and fractional Pareto optimality (fPO) for arbitrary additive valuations. This generalizes a recent result of Kawase et al. (2026), which establishes existence only for personalized bivalued valuations or when there are at most two distinct valuation types. Our proof applies the Knaster-Kuratowski-Mazurkiewicz (KKM) lemma to a weighted-welfare duality framework and develops a novel price-interlacing lemma to overcome barriers encountered by prior work. We extend this technique to category constraints, also known as partition-matroid constraints. In this setting, we establish the existence of an fPO allocation satisfying a weaker, category-sensitive relaxation of EF1, under which envy can be eliminated by removing at most one good from each category. All proofs in this paper were obtained using GPT-5.6-Sol with guidance from the authors. The authors verified the proofs, expanded the exposition, and simplified the arguments with assistance from GPT-5.6-Sol and Claude Fable 5.

</details>


### [287] [Resourced Authority A Mechanism-Design Model for Participatory Governance of Deployed AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06353)
*Praphul Chandra,Sujit Gujar,Ganesh Ghalme*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于已部署AI代理的持续参与式治理的形式化机制设计模型。该机制通过资源分配来控制AI代理，利用计算预算实现授权的自我执行，建立了"计算是有效治理杠杆"的Safe AI范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理被部署到现实世界环境中，如何对其进行持续的、参与式的治理成为一个关键问题。现有的治理方法缺乏正式机制设计基础，且难以实现授权的自动执行。作者试图建立一种基于计算资源分配的形式化治理机制，使得治理决策能够自我执行。

Method: 该机制将治理构建为部署者之上的合规或公共资源覆盖层。治理周期被建模为扩展形式博弈：经认证的人类利益相关者按顺序到达，在供给或拒绝市场中以与代理计算分离的治理货币做出贡献。资金聚合器将原始贡献转化为广度加权有效支持；具有滞回特性的双阈值门将净支持转化为二元授权；通过受外部认证安全上限约束的耦合映射，释放计量计算预算——在硬件层面实现为有符号计算许可证，使决策自我执行。

Result: 论文刻画了该机制所能治理的代理类别，并将"被治理代理对治理选民的操纵"识别为核心开放问题。作者还提出了若干针对被治理代理操纵治理选民这一挑战的应对方案。

Conclusion: 论文确立了一种计算预算驱动的自我执行治理机制的形式化基础，将资源分配作为治理AI代理的主要杠杆，同时承认并界定了治理选民的操纵风险这一开放挑战。

Abstract: We give a formal mechanism design model for the continuous participatory governance of a deployed AI agent. The mechanism is built on the principle that governance should control an AI agent through resource allocation so as to make authorization self enforcing via compute budgets. The mechanism seeks to establish the Safe AI paradigm that compute is an effective governance lever. We situate our work as a compliance or commons overlay on a deployer. One governance period is an extensive form game in which verified human stakeholders arrive sequentially and contribute, on a provision or a rejection market, in a governance currency that is deliberately distinct from the agents compute. A funding aggregator turns raw contributions into breadth weighted effective supports - a two threshold gate with hysteresis converts net support into a binary authorization that, through a coupling map bounded by an exogenously certified safety ceiling, releases a metered compute budget - realized in hardware as a signed compute license so that the decision is self-enforcing. We characterize the class of agents the mechanism can govern and isolate manipulation of the governing electorate by the governed agent as the central open problem. We also introduce several challenges addressing manipulation of governing electorate by the governed agents.

</details>


### [288] [AV-AIVAT: 74x Cheaper Agent Evaluation with Certified Anytime-Valid Stopping in Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06362)
*Boning Li,Yu Chen,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出AV-AIVAT，将AIVAT方差缩减技术与随时有效的置信序列（CSs）相结合，使不完全信息博弈中"两个智能体谁更强"的评估能在证据充分时立即停止，且保证统计显著性不被破坏。相比原始结果，AIVAT修正结果在停止前需要的中位手牌数大幅减少（AsympCS下约1/74），并实现了精确有限样本认证与可审计的早期停止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断两个博弈智能体的强弱需要反复对局直到技巧战胜运气，但每局游戏都消耗金钱或推理资源。由于所需对局数未知，固定预算评估要么在结论已定后继续浪费资源，要么在能区分两者之前就过早停止；而朴素的可选停止配合普通置信区间会破坏声称的置信水平。因此需要一个能尽早停止且统计保证完整的评估方法。

Method: 将AIVAT（通过条件零均值修正降低不完全信息博弈方差）与持续监测的置信序列（CSs）结合成AV-AIVAT。其在线价值模型仅从历史对局中学习，确保每局博弈不对自己的修正打分。使用渐近CS（AsympCS）配合AIVAT修正结果；精确有限样本认证采用经验-伯恩斯坦CS（EB-CS），需要独立论证的修正收益上界——该界在Leduc扑克中从结构上确立，并刻画了由CS下注上限和该界决定的宽度下限。

Result: 在95%名义水平、±1大盲注目标精度下，原始结果需要的中位手牌数为AIVAT修正结果的74倍（AsympCS下）。AIVAT在15种LLM智能体配置、71,439手HUNL对局中实现中位54倍方差缩减。描述性HUNL EB-CS实验显示中位停止时间比为1.37倍。

Conclusion: AV-AIVAT将方差缩减转化为高效且可审计的早期停止，同时将渐近筛选与精确认证分离，使评估能够在证据充分的瞬间停止，并向第三方移交在停止时刻重新核查结论所需的一切信息。

Abstract: Deciding which of two agents is stronger means playing games until skill outweighs luck, and every game costs money, model inference, or expert time. Since the number of games needed is unknown, fixed-budget evaluations either keep paying after the result is settled or stop before the agents can be told apart, while naive optional stopping with an ordinary confidence interval invalidates the stated level. We make such an evaluation stop as soon as its evidence suffices, with the guarantee intact. The Action-Informed Value Assessment Tool (AIVAT) reduces variance in imperfect-information games through conditional mean-zero corrections, by a median $54\times$ across 15 LLM agent configurations spanning 71,439 paired Heads-Up No-Limit Hold'em (HUNL) hands, but does not say when to stop. We combine AIVAT with continuously monitored Confidence Sequences (CSs) into anytime-valid AIVAT (AV-AIVAT), whose online value model learns only from past games so that no game scores its own correction. At the nominal 95\% level and a target precision of $\pm1$ Big Blind, raw outcomes need a median $74\times$ as many hands as AIVAT-corrected outcomes to stop under the Asymptotic CS (AsympCS). Exact finite-sample certification uses the Empirical-Bernstein CS (EB-CS), which needs an independently justified bound on corrected payoffs. We establish such a bound structurally for Leduc hold'em and characterize a width floor set by the CS's bet cap and that bound, which governs how much of a variance gain becomes earlier stopping; the descriptive HUNL EB-CS runs show a median $1.37\times$ stopping-time ratio. AV-AIVAT turns variance reduction into efficient, auditable early stopping while separating asymptotic screening from exact certification, so an evaluation can stop the moment its evidence suffices and hand a third party everything needed to recheck the verdict at that very stopping time.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [289] [MS-MLB: An Open Machine Learning Benchmark for Blood-Based MS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05196)
*Adam Simson,Ankush Dutta,Quang Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MS-MLB，一个可复现的开源机器学习基准，用于从全血RNA表达数据（GSE17048队列）进行多发性硬化（MS）与健康对照的分类研究。在泄漏控制的统一管道下评估多种算法，最终梯度提升在保持集上以MS研究评分93.83、AUC-ROC 0.989等指标排名第一。该基准提供文档化的外部模型提交通路，但评分仅用于研究比较，未经临床验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多发性硬化的诊断依赖临床评估、MRI、实验室证据等综合手段，血RNA表达数据虽可能包含疾病相关的免疫信号，但需要一个可复现、泄漏受控的公共基准来评估基于机器学习的MS分类研究，并规范外部模型的提交流程。

Method: 基于GSE17048公开数据集构建MS与健康对照分类任务，在统一、防泄漏的管道下评估多个算法。评估流程包括嵌套交叉验证、未触碰的分层保留集、bootstrap置信区间、ROC与精确率-召回率分析、校准测量，以及探索性的MS研究评分。框架内置文档化的外部模型提交通路。

Result: 在最终基准汇总中，梯度提升算法按保持集上的MS研究评分排名第一，取得MS研究评分93.83、AUC-ROC 0.989、灵敏度0.950、特异度0.778、F1分数0.927、Brier分数0.050。

Conclusion: MS-MLB是首个聚焦于GSE17048全血RNA表达数据MS与健康对照分类的开源基准，内置外部模型提交通路。其评分仅用于研究比较，尚未经过临床验证，不能替代临床诊断。

Abstract: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is diagnosed through clinical assessment, magnetic resonance imaging, laboratory evidence when appropriate, and exclusion of better explanations. Blood RNA expression data may contain disease associated immune signal, but a blood RNA classifier cannot be treated as a replacement for clinical diagnosis. This paper presents MS-MLB (Multiple Sclerosis Machine Learning Benchmark), a reproducible open benchmark for machine learning based MS research classification from whole blood RNA expression data. MS-MLB uses the public GSE17048 cohort, converts it into an MS versus healthy control task, and evaluates multiple algorithms under a shared, leakage controlled pipeline that a researcher can rerun without reconfiguring the evaluation. The evaluation includes nested cross-validation, an untouched stratified holdout set, bootstrap confidence intervals, ROC and precision recall analysis, calibration measurement, and an exploratory MS Research Score. In the final benchmark summary, Gradient Boosting ranked first by MS Research Score on the holdout set, with an MS Research Score of 93.83, AUC-ROC of 0.989, sensitivity of 0.950, specificity of 0.778, $F_{1}$ score of 0.927, and Brier score of 0.050. Prior studies have applied machine learning to MS blood transcriptomic data, including PBMC stage classification and whole blood diagnostic signature modeling. The contribution here is different and narrower. To our knowledge, MS-MLB is the first open benchmark focused on MS versus healthy control classification from GSE17048 whole blood RNA expression data with a documented external model submission pathway built into the framework. The score is intended for research comparison only and has not been clinically validated. The benchmark is accessible here: https://github.com/duckyquang/MS-MLB.

</details>


### [290] [Potential Matching Optimal Transport: Continuous Normalizing Flows for Exact $p$-Wasserstein Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05666)
*Lishuo Zhang,Ruizhi Huang,Yang Yu,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出势匹配最优传输（PMOT），一种面向一般p-代价最优传输的势流框架。PMOT用标量势参数化CNF速度场，通过自诱导匹配损失在直线桥上训练，建立零损失解与广义Benamou-Brenier最优性系统的严格对等关系，并在合成与高维数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优传输方法大多局限于平方代价（p=2），难以处理一般p-代价下的传输问题；同时需要同时满足终端分布匹配与传输成本最优的框架。作者希望构建一个统一、可扩展且具有理论保证的框架，能够处理任意p代价的OT问题。

Method: PMOT将连续归一化流（CNF）的速度场用广义Benamou-Brenier形式中的标量势参数化（针对任意指数p），然后用自诱导匹配损失在由模型自身端点确定的直线桥上训练势梯度，同时允许灵活的终端分布匹配。核心是建立零损失精确性定理：在正则性、精确终端匹配和唯一性假设下，任何零损失解都满足广义Benamou-Brenier最优性系统，并恢复对应的p-最优传输映射与动力学。

Result: 在合成基准上，PMOT学习到的p-特定映射与相应的p-匹配OT参考一致；在高维表格数据上作为基于似然的密度模型保持竞争力；MMD的颜色变换实验展示了灵活的基于样本的终端匹配能力。

Conclusion: PMOT为一般p-代价最优传输提供了理论上有保证、实践中有效的势流框架，兼具最优输运的理论精确性与CNF的灵活建模能力。

Abstract: We introduce Potential Matching Optimal Transport (PMOT), a potential-flow framework for general $p$-cost optimal transport with $c_p(x,y)=\|x-y\|^p$. PMOT parameterizes the CNF velocity field with a scalar potential in the generalized Benamou--Brenier form for the chosen exponent $p$. It trains the potential gradient with a self-induced matching loss along straight bridges determined by the model's own endpoints, while allowing flexible terminal distribution matching. Our main result establishes zero-loss exactness: under the stated regularity, exact terminal matching, and uniqueness assumptions, any zero-loss solution satisfies the generalized Benamou--Brenier optimality system and recovers the corresponding $p$-optimal transport map and dynamics. On synthetic benchmarks, PMOT learns $p$-specific maps that agree with the corresponding $p$-matched OT references. It also remains competitive as a likelihood-based density model on high-dimensional tabular data, and an MMD-based color transformation experiment demonstrates flexible sample-based terminal matching.

</details>


### [291] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFTER框架，通过挖掘冻结预训练预测器的残差来发现可解释的纠错特征，驱动轻量级事后纠错器。CRAFTER使用互补的双生成器（组合搜索和LLM）生成特征候选，经验证门控筛选后由纠错器应用。在六个数据集和六个冻结骨干网络上超越所有专用特征工程系统，特征预算下效果翻倍，将最弱骨干的误差降低27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的预训练预测器经常以结构性、可重复的方式失败，而通过微调修复代价高昂。已有自动特征工程方法建模的是数据生成过程，但纠错特征应建模模型失败过程。作者旨在通过挖掘残差中的可解释特征来纠正模型系统性错误，避免昂贵的微调。

Method: CRAFTER框架包含：(1) 两个互补的特征生成器——基于原始输入通道的组合搜索和基于LLM的命名特征组合、二元标志及可执行代码生成；(2) 单一验证集门控机制，对任一来源的特征候选进行接受或拒绝；(3) 验证集选择的轻量级纠错器，应用被接受的特征或保持预测不变。该框架是源无关的，允许在相同条件下评估已有的特征工程系统，从而将预测改进归因于特征来源本身。

Result: 在六个公共数据集和六个冻结骨干网络上，CRAFTER在所有特征预算下超越了每个专用的特征工程系统，比单独使用纠错器的改进大约翻倍，将最弱骨干网络的误差降低高达27%。这些增益在不同LLM后端间保持稳健，并且在微调后的骨干网络上依然有效。

Conclusion: 纠错特征发现（建模模型失败过程而非数据生成过程）是一种高效且可解释的方式，可在不微调的情况下修复冻结预测器的系统性错误。CRAFTER作为源无关的纠错框架，不仅自身性能优越，还可作为评估工具将预测改进归因于特征来源，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [292] [Alternating Levenberg-Marquardt Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks with Fourier-Enhanced Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05892)
*Yulun Wu,Matthieu Barreau,Miguel Aguiar,Karl H. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FALM-PINN框架，通过将表示学习与系数拟合解耦，利用傅里叶增强基和Levenberg-Marquardt算法解决PINN在高频、多尺度及强非线性PDE上的谱偏差和表示-系数耦合问题，在多个挑战性数值实验中相对L2误差比最先进基线低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在处理具有高频或多尺度解以及强非线性问题的PDE时经常失败，根本原因有两个：一是谱偏差（神经网络倾向于欠拟合高频特征），二是表示-系数耦合（表示学习与系数拟合在单一非凸优化目标中相互纠缠）。这两个问题严重限制了PINN的实际应用能力。

Method: FALM-PINN采用双层优化框架：上层问题学习一个傅里叶增强基，在潜在空间中引入高频分量以扩充表示能力；下层问题通过在上层学习的基上拟合投影系数来解耦耦合问题，使用Levenberg-Marquardt算法求解非线性最小二乘问题。该框架适用于一般非线性及耦合PDE系统，对于线性PDE可简化为单步凸优化问题。

Result: 作者在理论上证明了交替训练方案在非线性PDE和线性PDE两种情况下的全局收敛性。在多个高难度的含高频和非线性PDE数值实验中，FALM-PINN的相对L2误差比最先进基线低最多两个数量级。

Conclusion: FALM-PINN通过解耦表示学习和系数拟合，有效解决了PINN在高频和多尺度问题中的谱偏差及表示-系数耦合难题，兼具理论收敛性保证和显著优于现有方法的数值精度，为处理具有挑战性的PDE问题提供了一种稳健的优化框架。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) often fail to accurately resolve partial differential equations (PDEs) with high-frequency or multi-scale solutions, as well as strongly nonlinear problems. Two factors underlie this difficulty: spectral bias, the tendency of neural networks to underfit high-frequency features; and representation-coefficient coupling, the entanglement of representation learning and coefficient fitting within a single nonconvex optimization objective. In this work, we propose the Fourier-enhanced alternating Levenberg--Marquardt PINN (FALM-PINN), an optimization framework that decouples representation learning from coefficient fitting. The upper-level problem learns a Fourier-enhanced basis that enriches the latent space with high-frequency components, while the lower-level problem resolves the coupling by fitting the projection coefficients on this basis, solving a nonlinear least-squares problem with the Levenberg--Marquardt algorithm. The framework applies to general nonlinear and coupled PDE systems, and reduces to a single-step convex optimization problem for linear PDEs. We prove global convergence of the alternating training scheme in both cases. Numerical examples on multiple challenging high-frequency and nonlinear PDEs show that FALM-PINN achieves relative $L^2$ errors up to two orders of magnitude lower than state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [293] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于编程基于LLM流程的概率编程语言，使开发者能够量化和传播整个应用流程中的不确定性，并无需额外代码即可试验不同的推理缩放技术。作者通过实验研究和基于Rocq定理证明器的案例研究验证了该能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在各类任务中展现出强大能力，但其输出往往缺乏准确性和明确的置信度度量。当多个LLM调用和其他工具组合成复杂的流程时，这种不确定性会被放大，使得开发者和最终用户难以信任结果。因此需要一种方法来量化并传播流程中的不确定性。

Method: 提出一种用于编程LLM流程的概率编程语言，该语言支持：（1）在整个应用流程中量化和传播不确定性；（2）无需修改流程逻辑代码即可试验不同的推理缩放（inference scaling）技术。

Result: 通过实验研究展示了该语言的能力，并通过构建Rocq定理证明器的代理（agent）案例研究进行了验证。

Conclusion: 概率编程语言为构建可靠的LLM应用提供了一种系统化的方法，将不确定性管理作为流程编程的一等公民，有助于提升基于LLM应用的可信度和可靠性。

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [294] [Kastor: An efficient fine-tuning strategy for generative emulation of PDE simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06107)
*Guillaume Couairon,Alexis Jacq,Yu-Han Wu,Renu Singh,Yana Hasson,Quentin Berthet,Romuald Elie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kastor，一种将确定性物理基础模型改造为高效生成式代理模型的综合方法。通过两阶段推理（大步长自回归+非因果时间超分辨率）、均值预测正则化（MPR）和空间梯度匹配，显著降低误差累积并提升物理保真度。在The Well基准测试中，预测误差平均降低42.9%，且在10个数据集中有8个在VRMSE上优于基线方法Walrus。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习可用于加速物理仿真，但标准自回归ML模拟器存在两大问题：长时程推理中误差累积严重，以及难以捕捉复杂物理系统的随机性。因此需要一种既能保持精度又能刻画系统随机性的高效生成式代理模型。

Method: 提出三项核心技术：(1) 两阶段推理方案：大步长因果自回归模型配合非因果时间超分辨率网络，减少误差累积同时降低计算成本；(2) 均值预测正则化（MPR）：在零噪声条件下约束生成模型预测确定性分布均值，提升功能生成网络（FGN）和扩散模拟器的性能与稳定性；(3) 空间梯度匹配：提升预测精度和物理保真度，通过功率谱密度衡量。

Result: 在The Well基准的多个模拟数据集上，Kastor在预测精度、谱一致性和计算效率方面优于竞争方法。相比基于Walrus微调方法学的参照，预测误差平均降低42.9%，并在10个数据集中的8个上以方差归一化RMSE（VRMSE）指标超越Walrus。

Conclusion: Kastor通过结合两阶段推理、均值预测正则化和空间梯度匹配，成功构建了兼具高精度、高物理保真度和计算效率的生成式物理仿真代理模型，验证了将确定性基础模型改造为生成式模拟器的可行性。

Abstract: Machine learning offers a promising avenue to accelerate physical simulations by replacing computationally expensive traditional Partial Differential Equation (PDE) solvers with fast, differentiable surrogate models. However, standard auto-regressive ML emulators often suffer from error accumulation over long horizons and struggle to capture the stochasticity of complex physical systems. In this paper, we propose Kastor, a comprehensive methodology to adapt a deterministic physics foundation model into a highly efficient and accurate generative surrogate. First, we introduce a two-stage inference scheme that combines a large-stride causal auto-regressive model with a non-causal temporal super-resolution network, significantly reducing error accumulation while minimizing computational cost. Second, we present Mean prediction regularization (MPR), a novel training objective that constrains the generative model to predict the deterministic distribution mean under null noise conditioning. This regularization dramatically improves the performance and stability of both Functional Generative Networks (FGN) and diffusion-based emulators. Finally, we demonstrate that incorporating spatial gradient matching improves the accuracy and physical fidelity of the simulations as measured by power spectrum density. Extensive evaluations on diverse simulation datasets of the benchmark The Well show that with these components, our model outperforms competing methods in forecasting accuracy, spectral consistency, and computational efficiency. Our model achieves a 42.9% average reduction in forecasting compared to our reference based on the Walrus finetuning methodology, and outperforms Walrus for 8 out of 10 datasets on variance-normalized RMSE (VRMSE).

</details>


### [295] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CGTime，一个4B参数的计算接地时间序列-语言模型。通过将感知与描述解耦——用确定性代码从多变量时间序列中计算统计量，再让LLM将预计算结果言语化——解决了时间序列-语言对齐中的自我监督陷阱和多变量数据缺口两大问题。CGTime在多变量理解任务上超越了GPT-4o-mini和GPT-5.4-nano等更大规模的通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练多模态模型对齐时间序列与语言面临两个核心问题：(1) 自我监督陷阱——让LLM读取序列并生成描述，标签质量受限于模型本身的感知能力，数据永远无法教会模型超出标注者已知的内容；(2) 多变量数据缺失——大多数数据集仅使用单变量，但关键的跨通道相关性、超前滞后结构、共现异常等模式只出现在多变量场景中。这两个问题形成了"可靠、真实、可扩展"的三角困境，现有方法无法同时满足三者。

Method: 核心思想是解耦感知与描述。具体地：用确定性代码从真实开源多变量时间序列中计算一组统计量，再由LLM将这些预计算的事实进行言语化表达。感知（LLM不擅长的部分）完全交给计算处理，而LLM只负责描述/表达（其擅长的工作）。由此构建了4B参数的CGTime模型，数据来自真实开源多变量序列。

Result: CGTime在多变量理解任务上超越更大规模的通用模型：持出基准上的多变量事实分数达0.283，优于GPT-4o-mini（0.173）和GPT-5.4-nano（0.203），且在Holm校正的配对显著性检验中对所有基线均显著。此外，它在生成描述中陈述可验证数值事实的准确率更高，覆盖的统计属性范围更广。

Conclusion: 通过将感知与描述解耦，计算负责感知、LLM负责表达，可以有效规避自我监督陷阱和多变量数据缺失问题，用较小的模型实现超越更大规模通用模型的多变量时间序列理解能力。这为时间序列-语言对齐提供了一种同时满足可靠、真实、可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [296] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR)框架，将3D感知与空间推理显式解耦：利用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重构为结构化3D证据，再通过LoRA微调大语言模型仅基于这些显式几何证据进行推理，在空间推理基准上取得有竞争力的性能，同时提升了可解释性、模块化和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端方法通过大规模训练隐式地联合获得3D感知和推理能力，但作者观察到感知模型擅长估计连续3D几何，而大语言模型擅长组合与符号推理，二者优势互补，因此探索将感知与推理显式分离的替代范式，以规避大规模3D VQA训练和复杂工具使用策略的依赖。

Method: DiSR包含两个阶段：(1) 使用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化的3D几何证据；(2) 通过LoRA微调LLM，使其仅基于这些显式几何证据进行空间推理，无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂的工具使用策略。

Result: 在流行的空间推理基准上取得了与端到端方法相当的性能，同时提供了更好的可解释性、模块化和计算效率。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知与推理是空间智能中一种可扩展且有效的替代范式，证明了无需端到端建模也能实现强大的空间推理能力。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [297] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [298] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM框架，通过四阶段数据合成管道生成"画像-任务"配对、前缀引导规则集、质量过滤规则和结构化验证轨迹，将多模态大模型从"按规则被评分的生成器"转变为"遵循规则的执行器"。仅用10K合成样本即可将Qwen3-VL-4B在PRISM-Eval上的严格准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，且收益可迁移至四种开源多模态大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的多模态指令往往捆绑多个重要性不等的要求，但现有多模态训练数据大多将指令遵循简化为回答一个自包含的问题，模型缺乏对多规则、优先级感知指令的遵循能力，这一能力差距亟待填补。

Method: 提出PRISM四阶段数据合成框架：(1)生成画像-任务配对；(2)生成前缀引导的规则集；(3)质量过滤生成高质量规则；(4)产生结构化验证轨迹。同时提出PRISM-Eval评估基准，通过Loose和Strict两种指标对固定标签进行确定性匹配，无需推理时的判别模型。

Result: 使用仅10K合成样本，PRISM使Qwen3-VL-4B在PRISM-Eval上的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，同时保持通用基准的平均性能不降。该收益可迁移至四种额外开源多模态大模型（覆盖稠密和MoE架构）。

Conclusion: 结构化规则监督是通往多规则、优先级感知多模态指令遵循的可扩展路径，证明了规则理解任务与数据合成方式的可行性。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [299] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroSFT是一种参数高效的overfitting-aware多任务SFT框架，将原msft方法中的全量模型检查点状态重新表述为紧凑、可合并的adapter状态，冻结预训练主干网络仅训练注入的adapter，在五个backbone上平均准确率61.36%，超过msft参考行的59.85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务SFT将异构数据混合作为单一优化问题处理，但不同任务达到最佳泛化性能的时间不同。msft通过任务级roll-out、exclusion和rollback暴露了这一不匹配，但其原始实现将调度器状态物化为全模型检查点，导致阶段转换在存储、恢复和部署上代价高昂。

Method: AuroSFT冻结预训练主干网络，仅训练注入的adapter，在任务级峰值处回滚adapter检查点，并继续在剩余活跃任务混合上训练。在层级别，每个adapter采用AuroRA启发的自适应非线性层，作用于低秩权重因子而非样本表示，使得更新在输入上保持线性、秩有界，且可精确合并到冻结投影中。

Result: 在保留backbone的比较协议下，AuroSFT平均准确率达到61.36%，相比之下msft参考行为59.85%，并且在全部五个backbone上均获得更高的准确率。

Conclusion: AuroSFT通过将msft的调度状态压缩为可合并的adapter状态，在保持overfitting-aware多任务SFT收益的同时大幅降低了存储、恢复和部署成本，且在所有测试backbone上一致优于msft参考结果。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [300] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuroOFT，一种面向低比特语言模型的参数高效微调方法，在量化正交微调（QOFT）基础上附加零起始门控低秩非线性残差分支，使模型具备输入相关的非线性校正能力，同时在初始化时与QOFT功能等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化正交微调（QOFT）虽然能够实现低比特语言模型的参数高效适配，但其任务特定更新被限制在线性正交变换范围内，缺乏输入依赖的非线性校正能力，这限制了模型在复杂任务上的表达力。

Method: AuroOFT保留QOFT作为稳定的量化兼容主分支，同时为每个适配的线性层附加一个零起始门控低秩非线性残差分支。该分支将激活映射到RMS归一化的紧凑潜在空间，使用自适应非线性基和边界或令牌依赖的门控。零初始化的上投影确保AuroOFT在初始化时与QOFT功能完全一致，正交性仅作为分支级稳定性属性而非组合非线性层的属性。

Result: 在相同数据、优化、解码和解析协议下，AuroOFT在Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B设置上比匹配的QOFT提升Macro-6指标1.30-2.70%，超过QLoRA 6.52-10.62%，并相比QLoRA节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。

Conclusion: AuroOFT通过在QOFT基础上引入零起始门控低秩非线性残差，有效突破线性正交变换的表达限制，在不牺牲量化兼容性和稳定性的前提下，以更少的可训练参数获得显著的性能提升。小型选择题数学集仅作为协议敏感性诊断使用。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>


### [301] [SEAM: Global consistency beyond local accuracy in scientific machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05702)
*Gnankan Landry Regis N'guessan,Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SEAM（Scientific Explanation-Admissibility Machines），一个生成器无关的框架，用于检验科学机器学习模型中局部解释能否组装成全局一致的、可接受的科学解释。SEAM-$Ω$将每个子区域表示为带状态、闭包和观测通道的结构化解释，比较相邻解释的重叠并量化不一致性，将不一致性转化为通道分辨的障碍（obstruction），并支持精确可行性检验和残差感知的规则化归因。在19个实验中（含合成PDE系统和分布外FNO监控），SEAM能检测出局部预测准确但解释不兼容的情况，并将失败归因到特定通道和重叠区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习通常在子域、基准划分或单次预测解释层面验证模型，但这种局部检查无法确定所有局部解释能否组装成一个全局可接受的解释。现有方法缺乏从局部到全局的解释一致性计算框架，因此需要一个统一的、生成器无关的框架来回答这一局部到全局的相容性问题。

Method: SEAM框架包含：(1) 有限解释层丛（sheaf）实例SEAM-$Ω$，每个区域由带状态、闭包和观测通道的结构化解释表示，并附有可选的契约元数据；(2) 对相邻解释的重叠进行比较，将不一致性转化为通道分辨的障碍；(3) 通过限制修复范围为某声明账户允许的修订来测试竞争性账户，精确可行性验证可反驳或保留账户；(4) 当精确修复不可时，使用残差感知的规则化记录提供单独标记的经验归因；(5) 将不一致性与不可辨识性分离，并在分布偏移下监控学习生成器。理论方面建立了最小成本干预和守恒契约可检测性定理，以及可辨识性和闭包可恢复性的伴随结果。

Result: 在19个实验中，包括合成偏微分方程系统和分布外Fourier神经算子（FNO）监控，SEAM在局部预测准确时仍能检测到不兼容的解释，并将失败归因于具体通道和重叠区域。这证明SEAM能够为现有求解器和学习模型增加全局解释一致性审计能力。

Conclusion: SEAM为科学机器学习提供了一种全局解释一致性审计方法，测试局部解释是否形成连贯的科学说明。该框架在理论上有严格的定理支撑（最小成本干预、守恒契约可检测性、可辨识性、闭包可恢复性），实验上验证了其在不同科学建模场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning commonly validates models at the level of a subdomain, a benchmark split, or an explanation for one prediction. Yet such local checks cannot establish whether the resulting explanations can be assembled into one globally admissible explanation. We introduce Scientific Explanation-Admissibility Machines (SEAM), a generator-agnostic framework that makes this local-to-global consistency question computable across regions, sensors, regimes, and model components. The finite explanation-sheaf instantiation SEAM-$Ω$ represents each region by a structured explanation with state, closure, and observation channels together with optional contract metadata; compares neighboring explanations on their overlaps; and converts disagreement into a channel-resolved obstruction. This obstruction locates inconsistency and tests competing declared accounts by restricting each repair to the revisions that one account permits. Exact feasibility refutes or retains an account; when exact repair is unavailable, residual-aware regularized records provide a separately labeled empirical attribution. The framework also separates inconsistency from non-identifiability and monitors learned generators under distribution shift. We establish theorems for minimum-cost intervention and conservation-contract detectability, together with companion results for identifiability and closure recoverability. Across nineteen experiments involving synthetic partial differential equation systems and out-of-distribution Fourier neural operator (FNO) monitoring, SEAM detects incompatible explanations even when local predictions are accurate, and attributes failures to specific channels and overlaps. SEAM adds a global explanation-consistency audit to existing solvers and learning models, testing whether their local explanations form a coherent scientific account.

</details>


### [302] [An Emerging Retail Portfolio Management Application: Personalized, Tax-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Natural Language Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05255)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向散户投资者的智能投顾应用：通过FastAPI后端和Web仪表盘，用户用自然语言描述投资目标，系统将其路由到六个投资指令之一，并通过三阶段强化学习系统（自监督跨资产编码器、MoE分配策略、LoRA适配器）生成实时、经纪商集成的个性化税务感知投资组合建议。系统已通过Alpaca模拟交易API完成端到端集成测试，但尚未对真实用户开放，属于部署前应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散户投资者缺乏机构客户所享有的个性化税务感知投资组合管理服务：现有智能投顾采用静态、基于规则的资金配置，而机构级系统则需高额账户最低限额和复杂技术栈，个人投资者难以企及。本文旨在填补这一市场空白。

Method: 构建一个功能完整的应用系统，包括：1) FastAPI后端与Web仪表盘，支持自然语言投资目标输入；2) 将目标路由至六个投资指令之一；3) 三阶段强化学习推荐系统——自监督跨资产编码器、含可学习意图路由器的MoE分配策略、以及利用用户券商行为数据个性化推荐而无需重训共享模型的LoRA适配器。系统还包含多用户认证、预览后确认流程、每日邮件摘要和可审计操作完整性链。

Result: 系统功能完整，已针对Alpaca模拟交易API完成端到端集成测试；通过14天滚动前向回测（含自举置信区间）作为部署前初步验证；同时报告了实际工程经验教训，包括静默失效的集成路径、第三方API调用挂起问题，以及端到端经验验证相对于信任检查点元数据的重要性。

Conclusion: 该应用是一个诚实的部署前新兴应用，具备明确的完整部署路径；其工程经验对构建在外部实时数据源上的其他应用型强化学习系统具有泛化价值。

Abstract: Retail investors lack access to the kind of personalized, tax-aware portfolio management that institutional clients take for granted -- existing robo-advisors use static, rule-based allocation, and institutional-grade systems require account minimums and technology stacks unavailable to individual investors. We present a fully built, integration-tested application that closes this gap: a FastAPI backend and web dashboard that let a user describe an investment goal in plain language (e.g. "I want steady growth but need to sell some shares next month for a down payment"), routes that goal to one of six investment mandates, and produces a live, broker-integrated portfolio recommendation from athree-phase reinforcement learning system -- a self-supervised cross-asset encoder, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) allocation policy with a learned intent router, and a lightweight LoRA adapter that personalizes recommendations from an individual's revealed brokerage behavior without retraining the shared model.
